_txt=get=>({num:false,get});\nconst _num=get=>({num:true,get});\nfunction _numv(x){const v=parseFloat(x);return isFinite(v)?v:null;}\nfunction scopeSortKey(code){if(!code)return '';const [_txt(r=>(r.pol||'')+'\u2192'+(r.pod||'')),_txt(r=>r.carrier),_txt(r=>r.mode),_num(r=>rateSortVal(r)),_num(r=>_numv(r.transit)),_num(r=>_numv(r.ftO)),_txt(r=>r.from||'')];\n  case 'localcharge':return [_txt(r=>r.feeType),_txt(r=>r.mode),_num(r=>rateSortVal(r)),_txt(r=>r.from||'')];\n  case 'flat':return [_txt(r=>r.unit),_num(r=>rateSortVal(r)),_txt(r=>r.from||'')];\n}return [];}\nfunction sortRows(arr,def){const s=state.sort;if(!s||s.col==null)return arr;\n  const order=new Map(rows().map((r,i)=>[r.id,i]));let getter,num;\n  if(s.col==='ts'){getter=r=>(r.ts!=null?r.ts:order.get(r.id));num=true;}\n  else{const d=sortDefs(def)[s.col];if(!d)return arr;getter=d.get;num=d.num;}\n  const dir=s.dir;return arr.slice().sort((x,y)=>{\n    let vx=getter(x),vy=getter(y);const ex=(vx==null||vx===''),ey=(vy==null||vy==='');\n    if(ex&&ey)return order.get(x.id)-order.get(y.id);\n    if(ex)return 1;if(ey)return -1;\n    let c=num?(Number(vx)-Number(vy)):String(vx).localeCompare(String(vy),undefined,{numeric:true,sensitivity:'base'});\n    if(c===0)return order.get(x.id)-order.get(y.id);\n    return dir*c;});}\nfunction sortArrow(col){const s=state.sort;const on=s&&s.col===col;\n  if(!on)return '<span class=\"th-ar th-idle\">\u21c5<\/span>';\n  return s.dir>0?'<span else{s.col=col;s.dir=1;}\n  state.pageIndex=0;renderTableOnly();}\nfunction clearSort(){state.sort={col:null,dir:1};renderTableOnly();}\nfunction fmtTs(ts){const d=new Date(ts);if(isNaN(d))return class=\"ts-date\">${date}<\/span><span class=\"ts-time\">${tm}<\/span>`;}\nfunction enteredCell(r){return `<td class=\"entered-cell\">${r&&r.ts?fmtTs(r.ts):'<span dpShift(n){\n  if(DP.view==='months'){DP.y+=n;}\n  else if(DP.view==='years'){DP.decadeStart+=n*12;}\n  else{DP.m+=n;if(DP.m<0){DP.m=11;DP.y--}else if(DP.m>11){DP.m=0;DP.y++}}\n */\nfunction dpEdit(id){\n  dpClose();\n  const inp=document.getElementById(id);if(!inp)return;\n  inp.dataset.prev=inp.value;\n  inp.readOnly=false;inp.classList.add('editing');\n function opsPrimaryType(base){return PRIMARY_TYPE[base]||null;}
function opsTypesOf(base){return OPS_TYPES[base]?OPS_TYPES[base].slice():null;}
const OPS_SEP='::';
const OPS_LEAF_ROWS=0;
/* D2D has no source sheet — reuse DUP's column header (delivered-terms layout incl. HOÀN THÀNH) */
function opsBaseHeader(base){if(base==='FCF'){const h=(RAW['EFA']&&RAW['EFA'][0])?RAW['EFA'][0].slice():[];if(h.length>6)h[6]='FCF+';return h;}if(base==='DO')return ['BU','SALES','BRANCH','OPS','TERM','DO+','TYPE','C.CODE','SHIPPER','CNEE','LINER','AGENT','REF#','HBL NO#','MBL NO#','ATD','ETA','VESSEL NAME','VOYAGE','CONTAINER NO#','SEAL NO#','PRE-ALERT RECIEPT','MBL CONFIRMATION','HBL CONFIRMATION','FREETIME CONFIRMATION','FREETIME TYPE','MBL RELEASE','HBL RELEASE','MNF SUBMIT','AN SENDING','ATA','DO RELEASE','DAYS AFTER ATA','PICKUP DATE','RETURN DATE','DONE'];const src=(base==='D2D')?'DUP':base;return (RAW[src]&&RAW[src][0])?RAW[src][0].slice():['HOÀN THÀNH'];}
function isOpsBase(s){return OPS_BASES.includes(s)}
function isOpsStyleBase(b){return b==='EFA'||b==='FCF';}
function opsStripDone(s){return (typeof s==='string'&&(s.slice(-5)==='#DONE'||s.slice(-5)==='#PEND'))?s.slice(0,-5):s}
function opsParts(s){const p=opsStripDone(s).split(OPS_SEP);if(p.length===4&&OPS_BASES.includes(p[0]))return {base:p[0],mode:p[1],type:p[2],dept:p[3]};if(p.length===3&&OPS_BASES.includes(p[0]))return {base:p[0],mode:p[1],type:opsPrimaryType(p[0]),dept:p[2]};return null;}
function isOpsDone(s){return typeof s==='string'&&s.slice(-5)==='#DONE'&&!!opsParts(s);}
function isOpsLeaf(s){return typeof s==='string'&&s.slice(-5)!=='#DONE'&&s.slice(-5)!=='#PEND'&&!!opsParts(s);}
function opsPendKey(leaf){return leaf+'#PEND'}
function isOpsPend(s){return typeof s==='string'&&s.slice(-5)==='#PEND'&&!!opsParts(s);}
function opsStatusOf(s){return isOpsDone(s)?'done':(isOpsPend(s)?'pending':'active');}
function isOpsSheet(s){return !!opsParts(s);}
function isFlatSheet(s){const p=opsParts(s);return !!(p&&p.type&&FLAT_COLS[p.type]&&FLAT_COLS[p.type][p.dept]);}
function opsBaseOf(s){const p=opsParts(s);return p?p.base:s;}
function opsLeafKey(base,mode,type,dept){if(dept===undefined){dept=type;type=opsPrimaryType(base);}const pt=opsPrimaryType(base);if(!type||type===pt)return base+OPS_SEP+mode+OPS_SEP+dept;return base+OPS_SEP+mode+OPS_SEP+type+OPS_SEP+dept;}
function opsKeyOf(p){return opsTypesOf(p.base)?opsLeafKey(p.base,p.mode,p.type,p.dept):opsLeafKey(p.base,p.mode,p.dept);}
function opsDoneKey(leaf){return leaf+'#DONE'}
const KEY_DEPTORD='shoptrans_deptord_v1';
function loadDeptOrd(){try{const d=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY_DEPTORD));return (d&&typeof d==='object')?d:{};}catch(_){return {};}}
let DEPTORD=loadDeptOrd();
function saveDeptOrd(){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY_DEPTORD,JSON.stringify(DEPTORD));}catch(_){}}
function opsDeptOrdKey(base,type){const pt=opsPrimaryType(base);if(!type||type===pt)return base;return base+OPS_SEP+type;}
function opsDeptDefaults(base,type){if(type&&OPS_TYPE_DEPTS[type])return OPS_TYPE_DEPTS[type].slice();return (OPS_DEPTS[base]||[]).slice();}
function opsDeptsOf(base,type){const k=opsDeptOrdKey(base,type);return (DEPTORD[k]&&DEPTORD[k].length)?DEPTORD[k].slice():opsDeptDefaults(base,type);}
function addDept(base,type,name){
  name=String(name||'').trim().toUpperCase();if(!name)return;
  const cur=opsDeptsOf(base,type);
  if(cur.indexOf(name)>=0){if(typeof toast==='function')toast('Department already exists');return;}
  DEPTORD[opsDeptOrdKey(base,type)]=cur.concat([name]);saveDeptOrd();
  OPS_MODES.forEach(mode=>{const leaf=opsKeyOf({base:base,mode:mode,type:type,dept:name});const dk=opsDoneKey(leaf),pk=opsPendKey(leaf);[leaf,dk,pk].forEach(k=>{if(!DATA[k])DATA[k]=[opsColsFor(base,type,name,mode).slice()];if(typeof SHEETS!=='undefined'&&SHEETS.indexOf(k)<0)SHEETS.push(k);});});
  save();const p=opsParts(active);if(p&&p.base===base&&p.type===type)active=opsKeyOf({base:base,mode:p.mode,type:type,dept:name});
  renderSheet();
}
function deleteDept(base,type,dept){
  const cur=opsDeptsOf(base,type);if(cur.length<=1){if(typeof toast==='function')toast("Can't delete the last department");return;}
  const next=cur.filter(d=>d!==dept);DEPTORD[opsDeptOrdKey(base,type)]=next;saveDeptOrd();
  const p=opsParts(active);if(p&&p.base===base&&p.type===type&&p.dept===dept)active=opsKeyOf({base:base,mode:p.mode,type:type,dept:next[0]});
  renderSheet();
}
function reorderDept(base,type,from,to){
  const cur=opsDeptsOf(base,type).slice();const fi=cur.indexOf(from);if(fi<0)return;
  cur.splice(fi,1);const ti=cur.indexOf(to);cur.splice(ti<0?cur.length:ti,0,from);
  DEPTORD[opsDeptOrdKey(base,type)]=cur;saveDeptOrd();renderSheet();
}
function opsDefaultLeaf(base){const t=opsPrimaryType(base);const d=opsDeptsOf(base,t);return opsKeyOf({base:base,mode:OPS_MODES[0],type:t,dept:d[0]||'FCD'});}
function opsSheetLabel(s){const p=opsParts(s);if(!p)return disp(s);return disp(p.base)+' · '+p.mode+(opsTypesOf(p.base)&&p.type?' · '+p.type:'')+' · '+p.dept+(isOpsDone(s)?' · COMPLETED':(isOpsPend(s)?' · PENDING':''));}
function doneCol(sheet){const hdr=(typeof DATA!=='undefined'&&DATA[sheet]&&DATA[sheet][0])||(RAW[sheet]&&RAW[sheet][0])||[];let idx=-1;for(let c=0;c<hdr.length;c++){const t=String(hdr[c]).split(String.fromCharCode(10)).join(' ').replace(/ +/g,' ').trim().toUpperCase();if(t==='HOÀN THÀNH'||t==='DONE')idx=c;}return idx>=0?idx:hdr.length-1;}
/* register D2D base placeholder so the sidebar lists it */
if(!RAW['D2D'])RAW['D2D']=[opsBaseHeader('D2D')];
if(!SHEETS.includes('D2D'))SHEETS.push('D2D');

const EFA_CCD_HEADER=(function(){const ecd=opsBaseHeader('EFA');return EFA_CCD_SRC.map(k=>ecd[k]).concat(['ACCESS CHECK','TRUCK SCHEDULE','TRUCK COMPANY','PICKUP INFORMATION','STUFFING COMPLETION','GATEIN','CDS NO#','CDS DATE','CDS RESULT','TAX APPLICABLE','CLEARANCE DATE','CDS FEE PAYMENT','RETURN DOCS','DONE']);})();
const EFA_DCD_SRC=EFA_CCD_SRC.slice();
const EFA_DCD_HEADER=(function(){const ecd=opsBaseHeader('EFA');return EFA_DCD_SRC.map(k=>ecd[k]).concat(['SI SUBMIT','MBL APPROVAL','HBL APPROVAL','ATD','MBL RELEASE','HBL RELEASE','DONE']);})();
const EFA_FCD_SRC=EFA_CCD_SRC.filter(k=>k!==19&&k!==20&&k!==21&&k!==22);
const EFA_FCD_FROM_CCD=EFA_FCD_SRC.map(k=>EFA_CCD_SRC.indexOf(k));
const EFA_FCD_HEADER=(function(){const ecd=opsBaseHeader('EFA');return EFA_FCD_SRC.map(k=>ecd[k]).concat(['EXPENSE DEBIT','EXPENSE PAYMENT','EXPENSE INVOICE','INCOME DEBIT','INCOME RECEIPT','INCOME INVOICE','TAX CONFIRM','DONE']);})();
const FCF_DCD_SRC=(function(){const a=[];for(let k=0;k<27;k++)a.push(k);return a;})();
const FCF_DCD_HEADER=(function(){const b=opsBaseHeader('FCF');return FCF_DCD_SRC.map(k=>b[k]).concat(['SI SUBMIT','MBL APPROVAL','HBL APPROVAL','ATD','MBL RELEASE','HBL RELEASE','DONE']);})();
const FCF_FCD_SRC=(function(){const a=[];for(let k=0;k<=18;k++)a.push(k);[23,24,25,26].forEach(k=>a.push(k));return a;})();
const FCF_FCD_HEADER=(function(){const b=opsBaseHeader('FCF');return FCF_FCD_SRC.map(k=>b[k]).concat(['EXPENSE DEBIT','EXPENSE PAYMENT','EXPENSE INVOICE','INCOME DEBIT','INCOME RECEIPT','INCOME INVOICE','TAX CONFIRM','DONE']);})();

const DO_FCD_HEADER=['BU','SALES','BRANCH','OPS','TERM','DO+','TYPE','C.CODE','SHIPPER','CNEE','LINER','AGENT','REF#','HBL NO#','MBL NO#','ATD','ETA','VESSEL NAME','VOYAGE','CONTAINER NO#','SEAL NO#','ATA','EXPENSE DEBIT','EXPENSE PAYMENT','EXPENSE INVOICE','INCOME DEBIT','INCOME RECEIPT','INCOME INVOICE','TAX CONFIRM','DONE'];
function opsLeafHeader(base,dept,mode){if(base==='DUP'){if(dept==='FCD')return DUP_FCD_HEADER.slice();const M=(mode==='LCL')?'LCL':'FCL';const set=DUP_HDR[M]||DUP_HDR.FCL;if(set&&set[dept])return set[dept].slice();}if(base==='DO'&&dept==='FCD')return DO_FCD_HEADER.slice();if(base==='EFA'&&dept==='CCD')return EFA_CCD_HEADER.slice();if(base==='EFA'&&dept==='DCD')return EFA_DCD_HEADER.slice();if(base==='EFA'&&dept==='FCD')return EFA_FCD_HEADER.slice();if(base==='FCF'&&dept==='DCD')return FCF_DCD_HEADER.slice();if(base==='FCF'&&dept==='FCD')return FCF_FCD_HEADER.slice();return opsBaseHeader(base);}
function opsColsFor(base,type,dept,mode){if(type&&FLAT_COLS[type]&&FLAT_COLS[type][dept])return FLAT_COLS[type][dept].slice();return opsLeafHeader(base,dept,mode);}
(function migrateGsdDept(){
  var FLAG='shoptrans_gsd_v1';
  try{if(localStorage.getItem(FLAG))return;}catch(_){}
  var changed=false;
  OPS_BASES.forEach(function(base){
    var types=opsTypesOf(base);if(!types)return;
    types.forEach(function(type){
      var def=opsDeptDefaults(base,type);if(def[0]!=='GSD')return;
      var k=opsDeptOrdKey(base,type),cur=DEPTORD[k];
      if(cur&&cur.length&&cur.indexOf('GSD')<0){DEPTORD[k]=['GSD'].concat(cur);changed=true;}
    });
  });
  if(changed)saveDeptOrd();
  try{localStorage.setItem(FLAG,'1');}catch(_){}
})();
OPS_BASES.forEach(base=>{
  const _types=opsTypesOf(base)||[null];OPS_MODES.forEach(mode=>_types.forEach(type=>opsDeptsOf(base,type).forEach(dept=>{
    const hdr=opsColsFor(base,type,dept,mode);const ncol=hdr.length;
    const leaf=type?opsLeafKey(base,mode,type,dept):opsLeafKey(base,mode,dept);
    if(!(leaf in RAW)){const g=[hdr.slice()];for(let i=0;i<OPS_LEAF_ROWS;i++)g.push(new Array(ncol).fill(''));RAW[leaf]=g;}
    if(!SHEETS.includes(leaf))SHEETS.push(leaf);
    const dk=opsDoneKey(leaf);
    if(!(dk in RAW))RAW[dk]=[hdr.slice()];
    if(!SHEETS.includes(dk))SHEETS.push(dk);
    const pk=opsPendKey(leaf);

  })));
});

function tsPhys(sheet){const a=(XADD&&XADD[sheet])?XADD[sheet]:[];const e=a.find(x=>x&&x.ts);return e?e.c:-1;}
function nowTS(){const d=new Date(),p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');return d.getFullYear()+'-'+p(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+p(d.getDate())+' '+p(d.getHours())+':'+p(d.getMinutes());}
function ensureTS(sheet){
  if(!DATA[sheet]||!Array.isArray(DATA[sheet]))return -1;
  let ph=tsPhys(sheet);
  if(ph>=0){const h=DATA[sheet][0];if(h){while(h.length<=ph)h.push('');if(!h[ph])h[ph]='TIME';}return ph;}
  const n0=normalize(sheet);
  const vc=(typeof efaVisCols==='function')?efaVisCols(sheet):0;
  const phys=(vc>0)?Math.max(n0,vc+1):n0;
  DATA[sheet].forEach(r=>{while(r.length<phys)r.push('');r.push('');});
  if(DATA[sheet][0])DATA[sheet][0][phys]='TIME';
  XADD[sheet]=XADD[sheet]||[];XADD[sheet].push({c:phys,front:true,ts:true});
  saveXAdd();if(typeof save==='function')save();
  return phys;
}
function stampTS(sheet,rowIdx,force){const ph=ensureTS(sheet);if(ph<0)return;const r=DATA[sheet]&&DATA[sheet][rowIdx];if(!r)return;while(r.length<=ph)r.push('');if(force||!r[ph])r[ph]=nowTS();}

function deptLabelOf(sheet){const p=opsParts(sheet);return p?p.dept:sheet;}
function nextDeptSheet(sheet){const p=opsParts(sheet);if(!p)return null;const ds=opsDeptsOf(p.base,p.type);const i=ds.indexOf(p.dept);if(i<0||i>=ds.length-1)return null;return opsKeyOf({base:p.base,mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:ds[i+1]});}
function xferKeyOf(row){return (row||[]).slice(0,6).map(v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim()).join('|');}
function xferFindRow(tgt,key){const rows=DATA[tgt]||[];for(let i=1;i<rows.length;i++){if(xferKeyOf(rows[i])===key)return i;}return -1;}
function xferTransfer(srcSheet,r){
  const tgt=nextDeptSheet(srcSheet);
  if(!tgt){toast('No next department tab');return;}
  const src=DATA[srcSheet][r];if(!src)return;
  const key=xferKeyOf(src);
  if(!key.replace(/\|/g,'')){toast('Row is empty — nothing to transfer');return;}
  if(xferFindRow(tgt,key)>=0){toast('Already in '+deptLabelOf(tgt));return;}
  const copy=src.slice();const tc=xferCol(srcSheet);if(tc!=null&&tc<copy.length)copy[tc]='';
  const _sts=tsPhys(srcSheet);if(_sts>=0&&_sts<copy.length)copy[_sts]='';
  DATA[tgt]=DATA[tgt]||[[]];DATA[tgt].push(copy);normalize(tgt);stampTS(tgt,DATA[tgt].length-1,true);save();
  toast('Sent to '+deptLabelOf(tgt));
}
function xferUntransfer(srcSheet,r){
  const tgt=nextDeptSheet(srcSheet);if(!tgt)return;
  const src=DATA[srcSheet][r];if(!src)return;
  const key=xferKeyOf(src);const idx=xferFindRow(tgt,key);
  if(idx>0){DATA[tgt].splice(idx,1);save();toast('Removed from '+deptLabelOf(tgt));}
}
function buildColOrd(sheet,n){
  const users=opsUserSet(sheet);
  const hid=opsHideSet(sheet);
  const vc=efaVisCols(sheet);
  const full=ncols(sheet);
  const baseCount=(typeof n==='number'&&n>0)?n:((vc>0)?vc:full);
  let base=[];for(let c=0;c<baseCount;c++){if(efaHiddenCol(c))continue;if(users.has(c))continue;if(hid.has(c))continue;base.push(c);}
  const xs=(XADD&&XADD[sheet])?XADD[sheet]:[];
  xs.forEach(x=>{if(!x||typeof x.c!=='number')return;if(hid.has(x.c))return;if(vc>0&&x.c<=vc)return;if(x.front){if(base.indexOf(x.c)<0)base.unshift(x.c);return;}let idx=(x.after==null)?-1:base.indexOf(x.after);if(idx<0)base.push(x.c);else base.splice(idx+1,0,x.c);});
  return base;
}

function doMerge(){const rect=selRect();if(!rect){toast('Chọn vùng cần gộp trước');return}
  if(rect.r1===rect.r2&&rect.c1===rect.c2){toast('Chọn từ 2 ô trở lên để gộp');return}

}
function fmtOf(r,c){const m=FMT[active];const x=(m&&m[r+':'+c])||null;const sc=efaSrcCol(c);let def=null;if(isOpsSheet(active)&&isFlatSheet(active))return x||null;if(r>=1&&isOpsSheet(active)){if(isOpsStyleBase(opsBaseOf(active))&&sc>=0&&EFA_COLFMT[sc])def=EFA_COLFMT[sc];}if(!def&&r>=1&&isCCDSheet()&&CCD_DATE.has(c))def='date';if(!def&&r>=1&&isDCDSheet()&&DCD_DATE.has(c))def='date';if(!def&&r>=1&&isFCDSheet()&&FCD_DATE.has(c))def='date';if(!def&&r>=1&&isFcfDcd()&&FCF_DCD_DATE.has(c))def='date';if(!def&&r>=1&&isFcfFcd()&&FCF_FCD_DATE.has(c))def='date';if(!def&&r>=1&&isDoIcd()){if(DO_ICD_DATE.has(c))def='date';else if(DO_ICD_NUM.has(c))def='number';}if(!def&&r>=1&&isDupSheet()){const _dc=dupCfg();if(_dc.date.has(c))def='date';else if(_dc.num.has(c))def='number';}if(!x)return def?{type:def}:null;if(def&&!x.type)return Object.assign({},x,{type:def});return x;}
function parseDate(s){s=String(s).trim();if(!s)return null;
  let m=s.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{1,2})[\/\-.](\d{2,4})$/);
  if(m){let d=+m[1],mo=+m[2],y=+m[3];if(y<100)y+=2000;const dt=new Date(y,mo-1,d);return isNaN(dt)?null:dt}
  const t=Date.parse(s);return isNaN(t)?null:new Date(t)}
function fmtDate(d){const p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');return `${p(d.getDate())}/${p(d.getMonth()+1)}/${d.getFullYear()}`}
function parseTime(s){s=String(s).trim();if(!s)return null;let ap=null;const am=s.match(/(a\.?m|p\.?m)\.?$/i);if(am){ap=am[1].toLowerCase().charAt(0);s=s.replace(/\s*(a\.?m|p\.?m)\.?$/i,'').trim();}let h,m=0;let mt=s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*[:h.]\s*(\d{1,2})(?:\s*[:.]\s*\d{1,2})?$/i);if(mt){h=+mt[1];m=+mt[2];}else{let only=s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*h?$/i);if(only){h=+only[1];m=0;}else{let hm=s.match(/^(\d{3,4})$/);if(hm){const d=hm[1];if(d.length===3){h=+d.slice(0,1);m=+d.slice(1);}else{h=+d.slice(0,2);m=+d.slice(2);}}else return null;}}if(ap){if(ap==='p'&&h<12)h+=12;if(ap==='a'&&h===12)h=0;}if(isNaN(h)||isNaN(m)||h<0||h>23||m<0||m>59)return null;return{h:h,m:m};}function fmtTime(t){const p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');return p(t.h)+':'+p(t.m);}
function fmtDisplay(val,f){
  if(val==null||val==='')return '';
  const s=String(val);if(!f||!f.type||f.type==='text')return s;
  if(f.type==='number'){const num=Number(s.replace(/,/g,''));return(s.trim()!==''&&!isNaN(num))?num.toLocaleString('en-US'):s}
  if(f.type==='date'){const d=parseDate(s);return d?fmtDate(d):s}
  if(f.type==='time'){const tm=parseTime(s);return tm?fmtTime(tm):s}
  return s;
}
function cellAlign(f){if(typeof isEFASheet==='function'&&isEFASheet())return 'center';return f&&f.align?f.align:(f&&f.type==='number'?'right':'center')}
function setFmt(prop,val){
  let cells=[];const rect=selRect();
  if(rect){for(let r=rect.r1;r<=rect.r2;r++)for(let c=rect.c1;c<=rect.c2;c++)cells.push([r,c])}
  else if(lastRC)cells.push([lastRC.r,lastRC.c]);
  if(!cells.length){toast('Chọn ô (hoặc bấm vào 1 ô) trước');return}
  if(!FMT[active])FMT[active]={};
  cells.forEach(([r,c])=>{const k=r+':'+c;FMT[active][k]=FMT[active][k]||{};if(val==null)delete FMT[active][k][prop];else FMT[active][k][prop]=val;});
  saveF();const ed=$('#ssheet').querySelector('td.cell[contenteditable="true"]');if(ed)ed.blur();renderSheet();
}

/* ---------- SIDEBAR ---------- */
function renderSidebar(){
  const used=new Set();
  let html='';
  const blocks=GROUPS.map(([g,list])=>{
    const items=list.filter(s=>SHEETS.includes(s));items.forEach(s=>used.add(s));return[g,items];
  });
  for(const[g,items]of blocks){
    if(!items.length)continue;
    const collapsed=COLLAPSED.has(g);
    html+=`<div class="grouplbl ${collapsed?'collapsed':''}" data-group="${esc(g)}"><span class="gl">${gIcon(GROUP_ICON[g])}<span>${esc(g)}</span></span><svg class="chev" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6"/></svg></div>`;
    if(collapsed)continue;
    let clEmitted=false;
    for(const s of items){
      if(CHECKLIST_SET.has(s)){
        if(clEmitted)continue;
        clEmitted=true;
        const on=page==='sheet'&&isCklTab(active);
        html+=`<div class="sheet-item ${on?'active':''}" data-checklist="1"><span class="nm">CHECKLIST</span></div>`;
        continue;
      }
      if(isOpsBase(s)){
        const ek='OPSF:'+s,exp=OPS_OPEN.has(ek),p=opsParts(active);
        const pa=page==='sheet'&&((p&&p.base===s)||active===s);
        html+=`<div class="sheet-item opsfolder ${pa?'active':''} ${exp?'exp':''}" data-group="${ek}" title="Click the arrow or double-click to expand / collapse"><span class="nm">${dispHtml(s)}</span><svg class="opschev" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6"/></svg></div>`;
        if(exp)for(const m of OPS_MODES){const ma=page==='sheet'&&p&&p.base===s&&p.mode===m;
          html+=`<div class="sheet-item opsleaf ${ma?'active':''}" data-opsfolder="${esc(s)}" data-opsmodepick="${m}"><span class="nm">${m}</span></div>`;}
        continue;
      }
      if(s===MASTER_ANCHOR){const on=page==='sheet'&&MASTER_TABS.includes(active);html+=`<div class="sheet-item ${on?'active':''}" data-masterfolder="1"><span class="nm">${esc(MASTER_LABEL)}</span></div>`;continue;}
      html+=`<div class="sheet-item ${page==='sheet'&&s===opsBaseOf(active)?'active':''}" data-sheet="${esc(s)}"><span class="nm">${dispHtml(s)}</span></div>`;
    }
    if(g==='Master Data'){
      html+=`<div class="sheet-item ${page==='sales'&&salesSec==='fx'?'active':''}" data-page="sales" data-pid="fx"><span class="li">${ICON.db}</span><span class="nm">EX.RATE</span></div>`;
    }
  }
  $('#sheetlist').innerHTML=html;
}
$('#sheetlist').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const opf=e.target.closest('.opsfolder');
  if(opf){const g=opf.dataset.group||'';const base=g.slice(5);
    if(e.target.closest('.opschev')){OPS_OPEN.has(g)?OPS_OPEN.delete(g):OPS_OPEN.add(g);}else{OPS_OPEN.add(g);}
    page='sheet';active=base;visible=PAGE;query='';sel=null;lastRC=null;const _cs=$('#cellSearch');if(_cs)_cs.value='';
    showSheet();renderSidebar();renderSheet();return;}
  const chev=e.target.closest('.opschev,.chev');
  if(chev){const host=chev.closest('[data-group]');if(host){const g=host.dataset.group;
    if(g.indexOf('OPSF:')===0){OPS_OPEN.has(g)?OPS_OPEN.delete(g):OPS_OPEN.add(g);renderSidebar();}
    else{COLLAPSED.has(g)?COLLAPSED.delete(g):COLLAPSED.add(g);saveG();renderSidebar();}
    return;}}
  const pg=e.target.closest('[data-page]');
  if(pg&&pg.dataset.page==='sales'){
    page='sales';const pid=pg.dataset.pid,nav=pg.dataset.nav;salesSec=pid||nav;sel=null;renderSidebar();
    if(nav==='dust')showSales('DUSTBIN',w=>w.openTrash&&w.openTrash());
    else showSales(SALES_LABEL[pid]||'',w=>w.setPage&&w.setPage(pid));
    return;
  }
  const cl=e.target.closest('[data-checklist]');
  if(cl){page='sheet';active=isCklTab(active)?active:CHECKLIST_TABS[0][0];visible=PAGE;query='';sel=null;lastRC=null;$('#cellSearch').value='';showSheet();renderSidebar();renderSheet();return}
  const of=e.target.closest('[data-opsmodepick]');
  if(of){const base=of.dataset.opsfolder,mode=of.dataset.opsmodepick,type=opsPrimaryType(base),dept=(opsDeptsOf(base,type)[0]||'FCD');
    page='sheet';active=opsKeyOf({base:base,mode:mode,type:type,dept:dept});visible=PAGE;query='';sel=null;lastRC=null;$('#cellSearch').value='';showSheet();renderSidebar();renderSheet();return}
  const mf=e.target.closest('[data-masterfolder]');
  if(mf){page='sheet';active=MASTER_TABS[0];visible=PAGE;query='';sel=null;lastRC=null;$('#cellSearch').value='';showSheet();renderSidebar();renderSheet();return}
  const it=e.target.closest('[data-sheet]');
  if(it){page='sheet';active=it.dataset.sheet;visible=PAGE;query='';sel=null;lastRC=null;$('#cellSearch').value='';showSheet();renderSidebar();renderSheet()}
});
let salesLoaded=false,salesPending=null;
function salesNav(fn){
  salesPending=fn;const f=$('#salesframe');
  if(salesLoaded){flushSalesNav();return}
  if(!f.dataset.init){f.dataset.init='1';f.addEventListener('load',()=>{salesLoaded=true;styleSalesFrame();syncSalesCountry();flushSalesNav()},{once:true});
    try{f.srcdoc=JSON.parse(document.getElementById('salesHTML').textContent)}catch(err){toast('Không tải được trang nhập phí')}}
}
function flushSalesNav(){if(!salesPending)return;const fn=salesPending;salesPending=null;try{fn($('#salesframe').contentWindow)}catch(err){}}
function styleSalesFrame(){try{const d=$('#salesframe').contentDocument;if(!d||d.getElementById('opsHideChrome'))return;const st=d.createElement('style');st.id='opsHideChrome';st.textContent='.topbar{display:none!important}.sidebar{display:none!important}.shell{min-height:100vh!important}';(d.head||d.documentElement).appendChild(st)}catch(err){}}

  document.querySelector('.main').classList.remove('docs','regs');

}

function filteredRows(){
  const rows=DATA[active];
  if(!query)return rows.map((r,i)=>i);
  const idx=[0]; // always keep the header row
  for(let i=1;i<rows.length;i++){if(rows[i].some(c=>String(c).toLowerCase().includes(query)))idx.push(i)}
  return idx;
}
function renderChecklistTabs(){
  const host=$('#checklistTabs');if(!host)return;
  if(page==='sheet'&&isCklTab(active)){
    host.style.display='flex';
    let h=cklAllTabs().map(([s,lab])=>{
      const sheet=isCklSheet(s);
      return `<button class="cl-tab ${s===active?'on':''}${sheet?' sheet':''}" data-cltab="${esc(s)}">${esc(lab)}${sheet?` <span class="cl-x" data-clx="${esc(s)}" title="Delete tab">\u2715</span>`:''}</button>`;
    }).join('');
    h+=`<button class="cl-add" id="cklAddTab" title="Add a spreadsheet tab">+ Add tab</button>`;
    host.innerHTML=h;
  }else{host.style.display='none';host.innerHTML=''}
}
$('#checklistTabs').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const x=e.target.closest('[data-clx]');
  if(x){e.stopPropagation();cklDeleteSheet(x.getAttribute('data-clx'));return;}
  if(e.target.closest('#cklAddTab')){cklAddSheet();return;}
  const b=e.target.closest('[data-cltab]');if(!b)return;
  const s=b.dataset.cltab;if(s===active)return;
  active=s;visible=PAGE;query='';sel=null;lastRC=null;$('#cellSearch').value='';showSheet();renderSidebar();renderSheet();
});
$('#checklistTabs').addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('[data-cltab]');if(!b)return;if(!isCklSheet(b.dataset.cltab))return;cklRenameSheet(b.dataset.cltab);
});
async function cklAddSheet(){
  const def='Sheet '+(CKL_TABS.length+1);
  const name=await sysPrompt({title:'New tab',label:'Tab name',value:def,placeholder:def,okText:'Create tab'});
  if(name===null)return;
  const label=(name.trim()||def);
  const key='CKL_'+Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase();
  const cols=8;
  RAW[key]=cklBlankGrid(cols);
  if(!SHEETS.includes(key))SHEETS.push(key);
  RENAME[key]=label;
  DATA[key]=clone(RAW[key]);
  CKL_TABS.push({key:key,label:label,cols:cols});
  cklTabsSave();save();
  active=key;page='sheet';visible=PAGE;query='';sel=null;lastRC=null;$('#cellSearch').value='';
  showSheet();renderSidebar();renderSheet();
}
async function cklRenameSheet(key){
  const t=CKL_TABS.find(x=>x.key===key);if(!t)return;
  const name=await sysPrompt({title:'Rename tab',label:'Tab name',value:t.label,placeholder:t.label,okText:'Rename'});if(name===null)return;
  t.label=(name.trim()||t.label);RENAME[key]=t.label;cklTabsSave();renderSidebar();renderChecklistTabs();renderSheet();
}
async function cklDeleteSheet(key){
  const i=CKL_TABS.findIndex(x=>x.key===key);if(i<0)return;
  const ok=await sysConfirm({title:'Delete tab',message:'Delete this tab and all its data? This cannot be undone.',okText:'Delete',danger:true});
  if(!ok)return;
  CKL_TABS.splice(i,1);cklTabsSave();
  const si=SHEETS.indexOf(key);if(si>=0)SHEETS.splice(si,1);
  delete DATA[key];delete RAW[key];delete RENAME[key];
  save();
  if(active===key)active=CHECKLIST_TABS[0][0];
  renderSidebar();renderChecklistTabs();renderSheet();
}

/* ===== Editable checklist model (persisted, both tabs) ===== */
const CKL_KEY='shoptrans_ops_checklists_v2';
const CKL_DEFAULTS={'HQ':DOCS_CHECKLIST,'HQ-KTCN':IMEX_CHECKLIST};
let CKL_EDIT=false; let ckl_dirty=false; let CKL_FILTER='all',CKL_Q='';
function cklRid(){return Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6)}
function cklNormRow(r,base,ri){
  let kind='item',lead='',detail='',hot=0;
  if(r&&typeof r==='object'&&!Array.isArray(r)&&r.x!==undefined){kind='intro';lead=r.x;hot=r.hot?1:0;}
  else if(r&&typeof r==='object'&&!Array.isArray(r)&&r.h!==undefined){kind='subhead';lead=r.h;}
  else if(Array.isArray(r)){lead=r[0]||'';detail=r[1]||'';}
  else{lead=r==null?'':String(r);}
  return {id:base+'r'+ri,kind:kind,lead:lead,detail:detail,hot:hot};
}
function cklNormCard(c,base,ci){
  return {id:base+'c'+ci,title:c.title||'',rows:(c.rows||[]).map((r,ri)=>cklNormRow(r,base+'c'+ci+'_',ri))};
}
function cklBuildDefaults(){
  const m={};
  for(const k of Object.keys(CKL_DEFAULTS)){
    const b=k.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g,'')+'_';
    m[k]=CKL_DEFAULTS[k].map((c,ci)=>cklNormCard(c,b,ci));
  }
  return m;
}
let CKL_MODEL=null;
try{const _sv=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CKL_KEY));if(_sv&&_sv['HQ']&&_sv['HQ-KTCN'])CKL_MODEL=_sv;}catch(e){}
if(!CKL_MODEL)CKL_MODEL=cklBuildDefaults();
function cklSave(){try{localStorage.setItem(CKL_KEY,JSON.stringify(CKL_MODEL))}catch(e){}}
function checklistData(){if(!CKL_MODEL[active])CKL_MODEL[active]=[];return CKL_MODEL[active]}
function cklFindCard(cid){return checklistData().find(c=>c.id===cid)}
function ckSlug(){return active.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]/g,'')}
function cklCounts(){var D=checklistData(),slug=ckSlug(),done=0,total=0;var per=D.map(function(sec){var c=0,t=0;sec.rows.forEach(function(r){if(r.kind!=='item')return;t++;total++;if(DCKL_STATE[slug+'_'+r.id]){c++;done++}});return{c:c,t:t}});return{per:per,done:done,total:total}}
function cklNorm(x){return (x||'').toString().toLowerCase()}
function cklStripInner(){var cc=cklCounts();var pct=cc.total?Math.round(cc.done/cc.total*100):0;
  return '<div class="ckw-pct" id="ckwPct">'+pct+'<small>%</small></div><div class="ckw-bwrap"><div class="ckw-bar2"><i id="ckwBar" style="width:'+pct+'%"></i></div></div>';}
function cklRowsInner(){var D=checklistData();var per=cklCounts().per;var slug=ckSlug();var q=cklNorm(CKL_Q);var idx=0,shown=false;var left='',right='';var split=D.length>2;
  D.forEach(function(sec,i){var p=per[i];var rh='',pin='';
    sec.rows.forEach(function(r){
      if(r.kind==='item'){idx++;var gi=idx;var id=slug+'_'+r.id;var done=!!DCKL_STATE[id];
        if(CKL_FILTER==='pending'&&done)return;if(CKL_FILTER==='done'&&!done)return;
        if(q&&cklNorm(r.lead+' '+(r.detail||'')).indexOf(q)<0)return;
        var isCode=/^\u00d4\s/.test(r.lead);
        var lead=isCode?'<span class="ckw-lead"><b class="ckw-code">'+esc(r.lead)+'</b>'+esc(r.detail||'')+'</span>':'<span class="ckw-lead">'+esc(r.lead)+'</span>'+(r.detail?'<span class="ckw-rem">'+esc(r.detail)+'</span>':'');
        rh+='<div class="ckw-row'+(done?' done':'')+'" data-id="'+id+'" tabindex="0" role="checkbox" aria-checked="'+(done?'true':'false')+'"><span class="ckw-idx">'+('00'+gi).slice(-3)+'</span><span class="ckw-chk"><span class="ckw-box">'+DCKL_TICK+'</span></span><span class="ckw-cell">'+lead+'</span></div>';
      }else if(!q&&CKL_FILTER==='all'){
        if(r.kind==='subhead')rh+='<div class="ckw-subrow">'+esc(r.lead)+'</div>';
        else if(r.kind==='intro'&&(r.hot||cklNorm(r.lead).indexOf('xin hình ảnh')>=0||cklNorm(r.lead).indexOf('đối chiếu')>=0))pin+='<div class="ckw-noterow pin">'+esc(r.lead)+'</div>';
        else rh+='<div class="ckw-noterow">'+esc(r.lead)+'</div>';
      }
    });
    if(rh){shown=true;var pc=p.t?Math.round(p.c/p.t*100):0;
      var blk='<div class="ckw-sec" data-secidx="'+i+'"><span class="n">'+('0'+(i+1)).slice(-2)+'</span><span class="t">'+esc(sec.title||'Untitled')+'</span><span class="rule"></span><span class="sb"><i style="width:'+pc+'%"></i></span><span class="c">'+p.c+'/'+p.t+'</span></div>'+pin+rh;
      if(split&&i>=2)right+=blk;else left+=blk;}
  });
  if(!shown)return '<div class="ckw-empty">'+(q?'No items match \u201c'+esc(CKL_Q)+'\u201d.':(CKL_FILTER==='done'?'Nothing verified yet.':'All items verified \u2014 nothing pending.'))+'</div>';
  if(!split)return '<div class="ckw-col single">'+left+'</div>';
  return '<div class="ckw-cols"><div class="ckw-col">'+left+'</div><div class="ckw-col">'+(right||'<div class="ckw-colempty">No further sections</div>')+'</div></div>';}
function cklEditInner(){var D=checklistData();
  var out=D.map(function(sec,i){
    var rows=sec.rows.map(function(r){return cklRowEditHTML(sec.id,r)}).join('');
    var add='<div class="dckl-addrow"><button class="dckl-addbtn" data-add="item" data-cid="'+sec.id+'">+ Item</button><button class="dckl-addbtn" data-add="subhead" data-cid="'+sec.id+'">+ Subheader</button><button class="dckl-addbtn" data-add="intro" data-cid="'+sec.id+'">+ Note</button></div>';
    var head='<div class="ckl-ehead"><span class="ckl-ebadge">'+(i+1)+'</span><input class="dckl-titin" data-cid="'+sec.id+'" value="'+esc(sec.title)+'" placeholder="Section title\u2026"><span class="ckl-eact"><button class="dckl-ic" data-cmove="up" data-cid="'+sec.id+'" title="Move up">\u2191</button><button class="dckl-ic" data-cmove="dn" data-cid="'+sec.id+'" title="Move down">\u2193</button><button class="dckl-ic del" data-cdel="'+sec.id+'" title="Delete section">\u2715</button></span></div>';
    return '<div class="ckl-esec" data-card="'+sec.id+'" data-secidx="'+i+'">'+head+'<div class="ckl-ebody">'+rows+add+'</div></div>';
  }).join('');
  return out+'<div class="ckl-eadd"><button id="dcklAddCard">+ Add section</button></div>';}
function renderDocsChecklist(){
  const host=$('#docsChecklist');if(!host)return;const ed=CKL_EDIT;
  if(ed){host.innerHTML='<div class="dckl-head"><button class="dckl-editbtn on" id="dcklEdit">\u2713 Done</button><span class="dckl-edithint">Editing \u2014 changes save automatically</span></div><div class="ckl-acc edit"><div class="ckl-secs">'+cklEditInner()+'</div></div>';return;}
  var seg=function(k,l){return '<button data-ckwf="'+k+'" class="'+(CKL_FILTER===k?'on':'')+'">'+l+'</button>'};
  var bar='<div class="ckw-bar"><div class="ckw-search"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7"/><path d="m21 21-4.3-4.3"/></svg><input id="ckwQ" placeholder="Filter items\u2026" value="'+esc(CKL_Q||'')+'"></div><div class="ckw-tools"><button class="dckl-editbtn" id="dcklEdit">\u270e Edit</button><button class="dckl-reset" id="dcklReset" title="Clear all checks in this sheet">\u21ba Reset</button></div></div>';
  host.innerHTML='<div class="ckw">'+bar+'<div class="ckw-table" id="ckwBody">'+cklRowsInner()+'</div></div>';
}
function cklRowEditHTML(cid,r){
  const kindSel='<span class="dckl-kindsel">'+['item','subhead','intro'].map(k=>`<button class="dckl-kchip${r.kind===k?' on':''}" data-kind="${k}" data-cid="${cid}" data-rid="${r.id}">${k==='item'?'Item':k==='subhead'?'Subhead':'Note'}</button>`).join('')+'</span>';
  const showDetail=r.kind==='item';
  const showHot=r.kind==='intro';
  const ph=r.kind==='subhead'?'Subheader\u2026':r.kind==='intro'?'Note\u2026':'Item text\u2026';
  return `<div class="dckl-erow" data-erow="${r.id}" data-cid="${cid}"><div class="dckl-erow-main"><input class="dckl-lin" data-fld="lead" data-cid="${cid}" data-rid="${r.id}" value="${esc(r.lead)}" placeholder="${ph}">${showDetail?`<input class="dckl-din" data-fld="detail" data-cid="${cid}" data-rid="${r.id}" value="${esc(r.detail)}" placeholder="Hint / detail (optional)\u2026">`:''}</div><div class="dckl-erow-bar">${kindSel}${showHot?`<button class="dckl-hotbtn${r.hot?' on':''}" data-hot="1" data-cid="${cid}" data-rid="${r.id}" title="Emphasize">!</button>`:''}<span class="dckl-erow-act"><button class="dckl-ic" data-rmove="up" data-cid="${cid}" data-rid="${r.id}" title="Move up">\u2191</button><button class="dckl-ic" data-rmove="dn" data-cid="${cid}" data-rid="${r.id}" title="Move down">\u2193</button><button class="dckl-ic del" data-rdel="${r.id}" data-cid="${cid}" title="Delete">\u2715</button></span></div></div>`;
}
function dcklRefresh(){
  const host=$('#docsChecklist');if(!host||CKL_EDIT)return;
  const cc=cklCounts();const pct=cc.total?Math.round(cc.done/cc.total*100):0;
  var pe=host.querySelector('#ckwPct');if(pe)pe.innerHTML=pct+'<small>%</small>';
  var be=host.querySelector('#ckwBar');if(be)be.style.width=pct+'%';
  var me=host.querySelector('#ckwMeta');if(me)me.innerHTML='<b>'+cc.done+'</b> / '+cc.total+' items verified \u00b7 <em>'+(cc.total-cc.done)+' pending</em>';
  if(CKL_FILTER!=='all'||CKL_Q){var bd=host.querySelector('#ckwBody');if(bd)bd.innerHTML=cklRowsInner();return;}
  host.querySelectorAll('.ckw-sec').forEach(function(el){var i=+el.getAttribute('data-secidx');var p=cc.per[i];if(!p)return;var c=el.querySelector('.c');if(c)c.textContent=p.c+'/'+p.t;var sb=el.querySelector('.sb>i');if(sb)sb.style.width=(p.t?Math.round(p.c/p.t*100):0)+'%';});
}
function cklAfterAddFocus(cid){const card=document.querySelector('[data-card="'+cid+'"]');if(card){const ins=card.querySelectorAll('.dckl-lin');if(ins.length)ins[ins.length-1].focus();}}

  var __wf=e.target.closest('[data-ckwf]');if(__wf){CKL_FILTER=__wf.getAttribute('data-ckwf');var _bd=document.getElementById('ckwBody');if(_bd)_bd.innerHTML=cklRowsInner();document.querySelectorAll('.ckw-seg button').forEach(function(b){b.classList.toggle('on',b.getAttribute('data-ckwf')===CKL_FILTER)});return;}
  var __wr=e.target.closest('.ckw-row');if(__wr){var _id=__wr.dataset.id;if(DCKL_STATE[_id]){delete DCKL_STATE[_id];__wr.classList.remove('done');__wr.setAttribute('aria-checked','false');}else{DCKL_STATE[_id]=1;__wr.classList.add('done');__wr.setAttribute('aria-checked','true');}dcklSave();dcklRefresh();return;}
  if(e.target.closest('#dcklEdit')){if(CKL_EDIT){cklSave();ckl_dirty=false;}CKL_EDIT=!CKL_EDIT;renderDocsChecklist();return;}
  if(e.target.closest('#dcklReset')){const slug=ckSlug();Object.keys(DCKL_STATE).forEach(k=>{if(k.indexOf(slug+'_')===0)delete DCKL_STATE[k]});dcklSave();renderDocsChecklist();return;}
  if(e.target.closest('#dcklAddCard')){const dd=checklistData();dd.push({id:'c'+cklRid(),title:'New card',rows:[]});cklSave();renderDocsChecklist();return;}
  const cd=e.target.closest('[data-cdel]');if(cd){const id=cd.getAttribute('data-cdel');const dd=checklistData();const i=dd.findIndex(c=>c.id===id);if(i>=0){dd[i].rows.forEach(r=>{delete DCKL_STATE[ckSlug()+'_'+r.id]});dd.splice(i,1);}cklSave();dcklSave();renderDocsChecklist();return;}
  const cm=e.target.closest('[data-cmove]');if(cm){const id=cm.getAttribute('data-cid');const dir=cm.getAttribute('data-cmove');const dd=checklistData();const i=dd.findIndex(c=>c.id===id);const j=dir==='up'?i-1:i+1;if(i>=0&&j>=0&&j<dd.length){const t=dd[i];dd[i]=dd[j];dd[j]=t;cklSave();renderDocsChecklist();}return;}
  const ar=e.target.closest('[data-add]');if(ar){const cid=ar.getAttribute('data-cid');const kind=ar.getAttribute('data-add');const c=cklFindCard(cid);if(c){c.rows.push({id:'r'+cklRid(),kind:kind,lead:'',detail:'',hot:0});cklSave();renderDocsChecklist();cklAfterAddFocus(cid);}return;}
  const rd=e.target.closest('[data-rdel]');if(rd){const cid=rd.getAttribute('data-cid');const rid=rd.getAttribute('data-rdel');const c=cklFindCard(cid);if(c){const i=c.rows.findIndex(r=>r.id===rid);if(i>=0){delete DCKL_STATE[ckSlug()+'_'+rid];c.rows.splice(i,1);}}cklSave();dcklSave();renderDocsChecklist();return;}
  const rm=e.target.closest('[data-rmove]');if(rm){const cid=rm.getAttribute('data-cid');const rid=rm.getAttribute('data-rid');const dir=rm.getAttribute('data-rmove');const c=cklFindCard(cid);if(c){const i=c.rows.findIndex(r=>r.id===rid);const j=dir==='up'?i-1:i+1;if(i>=0&&j>=0&&j<c.rows.length){const t=c.rows[i];c.rows[i]=c.rows[j];c.rows[j]=t;cklSave();renderDocsChecklist();}}return;}
  const kc=e.target.closest('[data-kind]');if(kc){const cid=kc.getAttribute('data-cid');const rid=kc.getAttribute('data-rid');const kind=kc.getAttribute('data-kind');const c=cklFindCard(cid);if(c){const r=c.rows.find(x=>x.id===rid);if(r&&r.kind!==kind){r.kind=kind;if(kind!=='item')r.detail='';if(kind!=='intro')r.hot=0;cklSave();renderDocsChecklist();}}return;}
  const ht=e.target.closest('[data-hot]');if(ht){const cid=ht.getAttribute('data-cid');const rid=ht.getAttribute('data-rid');const c=cklFindCard(cid);if(c){const r=c.rows.find(x=>x.id===rid);if(r){r.hot=r.hot?0:1;cklSave();renderDocsChecklist();}}return;}
  const it=e.target.closest('.dckl-item');if(!it)return;const id=it.dataset.id;
  if(DCKL_STATE[id]){delete DCKL_STATE[id];it.classList.remove('done');it.setAttribute('aria-checked','false');}
  else{DCKL_STATE[id]=1;it.classList.add('done');it.setAttribute('aria-checked','true');}
  dcklSave();dcklRefresh();
});

/* ===== Operations: stacked tab bars (FCL/LCL/AIR · department · ACTIVE/COMPLETED) + row move ===== */
function opsDoneCount(leaf){const d=DATA[opsDoneKey(leaf)]||[];let n=0;for(let i=1;i<d.length;i++){if(d[i].some(x=>{const v=String(x).trim();return v!==''&&v!=='FALSE';}))n++;}return n;}
function opsPendCount(leaf){const d=DATA[opsPendKey(leaf)]||[];let n=0;for(let i=1;i<d.length;i++){if(d[i].some(x=>{const v=String(x).trim();return v!==''&&v!=='FALSE';}))n++;}return n;}
const KEY_ESEQ='shoptrans_ops_eseq_v1';
let ESEQ=(function(){try{const d=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY_ESEQ));if(d&&typeof d==='object')return d}catch(e){}return{};})();
function saveESeq(){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY_ESEQ,JSON.stringify(ESEQ))}catch(e){}}
function ensureESeq(sh){const n=(DATA[sh]||[]).length;let a=ESEQ[sh];if(!Array.isArray(a)||a.length!==n){a=[];for(let i=0;i<n;i++)a.push(i);ESEQ[sh]=a;saveESeq();}return a;}
function opsSortCol(sh,token){const hdr=(DATA[sh]&&DATA[sh][0])||[];token=token.toUpperCase();for(let c=0;c<hdr.length;c++){const h=String(hdr[c]==null?'':hdr[c]).toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();if(h===token||h.indexOf(token)>=0)return c;}return -1;}
function opsSort(mode){if(!isOpsSheet(active)||!mode)return;const rows=DATA[active];if(!rows||rows.length<=2){toast('Not enough rows to sort');return;}ensureESeq(active);const seq=ESEQ[active];const isEmpty=i=>rows[i].every(x=>{const v=String(x==null?'':x).trim();return v===''||v==='FALSE';});let col=-1;if(mode!=='new'){col=opsSortCol(active,mode==='etd'?'ETD':'ETA');if(col<0){toast('Column '+(mode==='etd'?'ETD':'ETA')+' not found');return;}}const key=i=>{const d=parseDate(rows[i][col]);return d?d.getTime():null;};const idx=[];for(let i=1;i<rows.length;i++)idx.push(i);idx.sort((a,b)=>{let r=0;if(mode==='new'){const ea=isEmpty(a),eb=isEmpty(b);if(ea!==eb)r=ea?1:-1;else r=seq[b]-seq[a];}else{const ka=key(a),kb=key(b);if(ka==null&&kb==null)r=0;else if(ka==null)r=1;else if(kb==null)r=-1;else r=ka-kb;}return r!==0?r:(a-b);});const newRows=[rows[0]];const newSeq=[seq[0]];const map={0:0};idx.forEach((oi,k)=>{newRows.push(rows[oi]);newSeq.push(seq[oi]);map[oi]=k+1;});if(FMT[active]){const nf={};Object.keys(FMT[active]).forEach(kk=>{const mm=kk.split(':');const r=+mm[0],c=+mm[1];const nr=(r in map)?map[r]:r;nf[nr+':'+c]=FMT[active][kk];});FMT[active]=nf;saveF();}if(MERGES[active]&&MERGES[active].length){MERGES[active]=[];saveM();}DATA[active]=newRows;ESEQ[active]=newSeq;save();saveESeq();sel=null;lastRC=null;renderSheet();toast(mode==='new'?'Sorted by newest entry':(mode==='etd'?'Sorted by ETD':'Sorted by ETA'));}
function renderOpsTabs(){
  const host=$('#opsTabs');if(!host)return;
  if(page==='sheet'&&MASTER_TABS.includes(active)){
    host.style.display='block';
    const row=MASTER_TABS.map(t=>`<button class="cl-tab ${t===active?'on':''}" data-mastertab="${esc(t)}">${esc(t==='STAFF'?'STAFF':disp(t))}</button>`).join('');
    host.innerHTML=`<div class="cl-tabs ops-row ops-row-master"><span class="ops-lbl">${esc(MASTER_LABEL)}</span>${row}</div>`;
    return;
  }
  if(!(page==='sheet'&&isOpsSheet(active))){host.style.display='none';host.innerHTML='';return;}
  const p=opsParts(active);const leaf=opsKeyOf(p);const st=opsStatusOf(active);
  const _types=opsTypesOf(p.base);
  const typeRow=(_types&&_types.length>1)?`<div class="cl-tabs ops-row ops-row-type"><span class="ops-lbl">Type</span>${_types.map(t=>`<button class="cl-tab ${t===p.type?'on':''}" data-opstype="${esc(t)}">${esc(t)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:'';
  host.style.display='block';
  const deptRow=opsDeptsOf(p.base,p.type).map(d=>`<button class="cl-tab dept-tab ${d===p.dept?'on':''}" data-opsdept="${esc(d)}" draggable="true"><span class="dept-lbl">${esc(tlGet('dept|'+p.base+'|'+d,d))}</span><span class="dept-x" data-deldept="${esc(d)}" title="Delete">\u00d7</span></button>`).join('')+`<button class="cl-tab dept-add" id="deptAddBtn" title="Add department">+</button>`;
  const cnt=opsDoneCount(leaf);const pcnt=opsPendCount(leaf);
  const statusRow=`<button class="cl-tab ${st==='active'?'on':''}" data-opsstatus="active">${esc(tlGet('status|active','ACTIVE'))}</button>`+
    `<button class="cl-tab ${st==='pending'?'on':''}" data-opsstatus="pending">${esc(tlGet('status|pending','PENDING'))}${pcnt?` <span class="ops-badge">${pcnt}</span>`:''}</button>`+
    `<button class="cl-tab ${st==='done'?'on':''}" data-opsstatus="done">${esc(tlGet('status|done','COMPLETED'))}${cnt?` <span class="ops-badge">${cnt}</span>`:''}</button>`;
  host.innerHTML=typeRow+`<div class="cl-tabs ops-row ops-row-dept"><span class="ops-lbl" data-deptword="1">${esc(tlGet('word|dept','Dept'))}</span>${deptRow}</div>`+
    `<div class="cl-tabs ops-row ops-row-status"><span class="ops-lbl">Status</span>${statusRow}<div class="ops-sort"><span class="ops-sort-lbl">Sort</span><select id="opsSort" class="opssort-sel" title="Sort"><option value="">\u2014</option><option value="new">Newest</option><option value="etd">By ETD</option><option value="eta">By ETA</option></select></div></div>`;
}

let _sysCb=null,_sysInputMode=false;
function sysAsk(opts){opts=opts||{};_sysCb=opts.cb||null;_sysInputMode=!!opts.input;
  const t=document.getElementById('sysTitle');if(t)t.textContent=opts.title||'';
  const wrap=document.getElementById('sysBodyWrap'),inp=document.getElementById('sysInput');
  if(opts.input){if(wrap)wrap.style.display='';if(inp){inp.placeholder=opts.placeholder||'';inp.value=opts.value||'';}}else{if(wrap)wrap.style.display='none';}
  const ok=document.getElementById('sysOk');if(ok)ok.textContent=opts.okText||'OK';
  const cn=document.getElementById('sysCancel');if(cn)cn.textContent=opts.cancelText||'Cancel';
  const ov=document.getElementById('sysOverlay');if(ov)ov.classList.add('on');
  if(opts.input&&inp)setTimeout(()=>{inp.focus();inp.select();},30);
}
function sysClose(ok){const ov=document.getElementById('sysOverlay');const inp=document.getElementById('sysInput');const v=inp?inp.value:'';
  if(ov)ov.classList.remove('on');const cb=_sysCb;_sysCb=null;
  if(cb){if(!ok)cb(null);else cb(_sysInputMode?v:true);}
}
(function(){const ov=document.getElementById('sysOverlay');if(!ov)return;
  ov.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.closest('#sysOk'))sysClose(true);else if(e.target.closest('#sysCancel'))sysClose(false);else if(e.target===ov)sysClose(false);});
  ov.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();sysClose(true);}else if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();sysClose(false);}});
})();
function startTabEdit(key,cur,host){
  if(!host)return;
  const inp=document.createElement('input');
  inp.type='text';inp.value=cur;inp.className='tab-edit';
  const old=host.innerHTML;host.innerHTML='';host.appendChild(inp);
  inp.focus();inp.select();
  let done=false;
  const finish=(commit)=>{if(done)return;done=true;if(commit){tlSet(key,inp.value);}renderOpsTabs();};
  inp.addEventListener('keydown',ev=>{ev.stopPropagation();if(ev.key==='Enter'){ev.preventDefault();finish(true);}else if(ev.key==='Escape'){ev.preventDefault();done=true;host.innerHTML=old;}});
  inp.addEventListener('mousedown',ev=>ev.stopPropagation());
  inp.addEventListener('click',ev=>ev.stopPropagation());
  inp.addEventListener('dblclick',ev=>ev.stopPropagation());
  inp.addEventListener('blur',()=>finish(true));
}
$('#opsTabs').addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{
  if(e.target.closest('.tab-edit'))return;
  const p=opsParts(active);
  const dw=e.target.closest('[data-deptword]');
  if(dw){e.preventDefault();startTabEdit('word|dept',tlGet('word|dept','Dept'),dw);return;}
  const dt=e.target.closest('.dept-tab');
  if(dt&&p&&!e.target.closest('.dept-x')){const d=dt.dataset.opsdept;const lbl=dt.querySelector('.dept-lbl');e.preventDefault();startTabEdit('dept|'+p.base+'|'+d,tlGet('dept|'+p.base+'|'+d,d),lbl);return;}
  const stb=e.target.closest('[data-opsstatus]');
  if(stb){const st=stb.dataset.opsstatus;const def={active:'ACTIVE',pending:'PENDING',done:'COMPLETED'}[st]||st;e.preventDefault();startTabEdit('status|'+st,tlGet('status|'+st,def),stb);return;}
});
$('#opsTabs').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const _mt=e.target.closest('[data-mastertab]');
  if(_mt){const t=_mt.dataset.mastertab;if(t&&t!==active){active=t;visible=PAGE;query='';sel=null;lastRC=null;const _cs=$('#cellSearch');if(_cs)_cs.value='';showSheet();renderSidebar();renderSheet();}return;}
  const _dx=e.target.closest('.dept-x');if(_dx){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();const _p=opsParts(active);const _dd=_dx.dataset.deldept;if(_p)sysAsk({title:'Delete department "'+_dd+'"? Its rows stay saved, but the tab is removed.',input:false,okText:'Delete',cb:ok=>{if(ok)deleteDept(_p.base,_p.type,_dd);}});return;}
  const _da=e.target.closest('#deptAddBtn');if(_da){e.preventDefault();const _p=opsParts(active);if(_p)sysAsk({title:'New department name',input:true,placeholder:'e.g. TCD',okText:'Add',cb:nm=>{if(typeof nm==='string'&&nm.trim())addDept(_p.base,_p.type,nm.trim());}});return;}
  const b=e.target.closest('[data-opstype],[data-opsdept],[data-opsstatus]');if(!b)return;
  const p=opsParts(active);if(!p)return;
  let type=p.type,dept=p.dept,st=opsStatusOf(active);
  if(b.dataset.opstype){if(b.dataset.opstype===type&&st==='active')return;type=b.dataset.opstype;dept=opsDeptsOf(p.base,type)[0];st='active';}
  else if(b.dataset.opsdept){if(b.dataset.opsdept===dept&&st==='active')return;dept=b.dataset.opsdept;st='active';}
  else if(b.dataset.opsstatus){const ws=b.dataset.opsstatus;if(ws===st)return;st=ws;}
  let key=opsKeyOf({base:p.base,mode:p.mode,type:type,dept:dept});if(st==='done')key=opsDoneKey(key);else if(st==='pending')key=opsPendKey(key);
  active=key;visible=PAGE;query='';sel=null;lastRC=null;$('#cellSearch').value='';showSheet();renderSidebar();renderSheet();
});
async function opsToggleDone(sheet,r,checked){
  const leaf=opsStripDone(sheet),done=opsDoneKey(leaf),dcol=doneCol(leaf),row=DATA[sheet][r];
  if(!row)return;
  if(isOpsPend(sheet)){row[dcol]=checked?'TRUE':'FALSE';save();return;}
  const onActive=(sheet===leaf);
  const hasData=row.some((v,i)=>i!==dcol&&String(v).trim()!=='');
  if(onActive){
    if(!checked){row[dcol]='FALSE';save();return;}
    if(!hasData){row[dcol]='FALSE';save();renderSheet();toast('Empty row — nothing to move');return;}
    const ok=await sysConfirm({title:'Mark as completed?',message:'Are you sure you want to move this row to the COMPLETED tab?',okText:'Yes, move'});
    if(!ok){renderSheet();return;}
    const moved=row.slice();moved[dcol]='TRUE';
    DATA[leaf].splice(r,1);DATA[leaf].push(new Array(ncols(leaf)).fill(''));
    if(!DATA[done])DATA[done]=[(DATA[leaf][0]||[]).slice()];
    if(!DATA[done][0])DATA[done][0]=(DATA[leaf][0]||[]).slice();
    DATA[done].splice(1,0,moved);
    save();renderSheet();renderOpsTabs();toast('Moved to COMPLETED');
  }else{
    if(checked){row[dcol]='TRUE';save();return;}
    const ok=await sysConfirm({title:'Move back to active?',message:'Move this row back to the ACTIVE tab?',okText:'Yes, move back'});
    if(!ok){renderSheet();return;}
    const moved=row.slice();moved[dcol]='FALSE';
    DATA[done].splice(r,1);
    DATA[leaf].splice(1,0,moved);
    for(let i=DATA[leaf].length-1;i>=1;i--){if(DATA[leaf][i].every(x=>String(x).trim()==='')){DATA[leaf].splice(i,1);break;}}
    save();renderSheet();renderOpsTabs();toast('Moved back to ACTIVE');
  }
}

function doDaysAfter(ri){const row=DATA[active][ri]||[];const ata=parseDate(String(row[30]||''));if(!ata)return '';const end=parseDate(String(row[33]||''))||new Date();const a=Date.UTC(ata.getFullYear(),ata.getMonth(),ata.getDate());const b=Date.UTC(end.getFullYear(),end.getMonth(),end.getDate());return Math.floor((b-a)/86400000);}
function doPush(srcSheet,r){
  const p=opsParts(srcSheet);
  if(!p||p.base!=='DO'||p.dept!=='ICD'||opsStatusOf(srcSheet)!=='active')return;
  const src=DATA[srcSheet]&&DATA[srcSheet][r];if(!src)return;
  const toKey=opsKeyOf({base:'DO',mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:'FCD'});
  if(!DATA[toKey])DATA[toKey]=[opsLeafHeader('DO','FCD').slice()];
  const map=DO_FCD_SRC,N=map.length;
  const srcIds=[12,13,14],toIds=srcIds.map(c=>map.indexOf(c));
  const norm=v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim().toUpperCase();
  const skVals=srcIds.map(c=>norm(src[c]));
  const hasId=skVals.some(v=>v!=='');const sk=skVals.join('|');
  const keyf=row=>toIds.map(c=>norm(row[c])).join('|');let target=-1;
  for(let i=1;i<DATA[toKey].length;i++){const row=DATA[toKey][i];
    if(hasId){if(keyf(row)===sk){target=i;break;}}
    else{let same=true;for(let j=0;j<N;j++){if(norm(row[j])!==norm(src[map[j]])){same=false;break;}}if(same){target=i;break;}}}
  const hdrLen=((DATA[toKey][0])||[]).length||N;
  if(target<0){const nr=new Array(hdrLen).fill('');for(let j=0;j<N;j++)nr[j]=src[map[j]]==null?'':src[map[j]];DATA[toKey].push(nr);}
  else{for(let j=0;j<N;j++)DATA[toKey][target][j]=src[map[j]]==null?'':src[map[j]];}
  save();if(typeof renderSheet==='function')renderSheet();if(typeof toast==='function')toast(target<0?'Sent to FCD':'FCD updated');
}
function doUntick(srcSheet,r){
  const p=opsParts(srcSheet);
  if(!p||p.base!=='DO'||p.dept!=='ICD'||opsStatusOf(srcSheet)!=='active')return true;
  const src=DATA[srcSheet]&&DATA[srcSheet][r];if(!src)return true;
  const toKey=opsKeyOf({base:'DO',mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:'FCD'});if(!DATA[toKey])return true;
  const srcIds=[12,13,14];const norm=v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim().toUpperCase();
  const skVals=srcIds.map(c=>norm(src[c]));const hasId=skVals.some(v=>v!=='');const sk=skVals.join('|');
  if(!hasId)return true;
  for(let i=1;i<DATA[toKey].length;i++){if(srcIds.map(c=>norm(DATA[toKey][i][c])).join('|')===sk)return false;}
  return true;
}
let csRow=null,csData=[];
function csRenderRows(){
  const tb=document.getElementById('csBody');if(!tb)return;
  if(!csData.length){tb.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="4" class="cs-empty">No entries yet — add one above</td></tr>';return;}
  tb.innerHTML=csData.map((r,i)=>`<tr><td><input class="cs-in" data-i="${i}" data-k="o" value="${esc(r.o||'')}"></td><td><input class="cs-in" data-i="${i}" data-k="c" value="${esc(r.c||'')}"></td><td><input class="cs-in" data-i="${i}" data-k="s" value="${esc(r.s||'')}"></td><td style="text-align:center"><button class="cs-del" data-i="${i}" title="Delete">\u2715</button></td></tr>`).join('');
}
function csPersist(){if(csRow==null)return;const clean=csData.filter(r=>String(r.o||'').trim()||String(r.c||'').trim()||String(r.s||'').trim());DATA[active][csRow][csCol()]=clean.length?JSON.stringify(clean):'';if(typeof save==='function')save();}

function csAddFromInput(){
  const go=document.getElementById('csInO'),gc=document.getElementById('csInC'),gs=document.getElementById('csInS');
  const o=go?go.value:'',c=gc?gc.value:'',s=gs?gs.value:'';

  csData.push({o:String(o).trim()||String(csData.length+1),c:String(c).trim(),s:String(s).trim()});
  csPersist();csRenderRows();csClearInput();if(go)go.focus();
}

(function(){const ov=document.getElementById('csOverlay');if(!ov)return;
  ov.addEventListener('input',e=>{const inp=e.target.closest('.cs-in');if(!inp||inp.dataset.i==null)return;const i=+inp.dataset.i,k=inp.dataset.k;if(csData[i]){csData[i][k]=inp.value;csPersist();}});
  ov.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){const inp=e.target.closest('.cs-in');if(inp&&inp.dataset.i==null){e.preventDefault();csAddFromInput();}}});
  ov.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(e.target.closest('#csSaveBtn')){csAddFromInput();return;}
    const del=e.target.closest('.cs-del');if(del){csDelRow(+del.dataset.i);return;}
    if(e.target.closest('#csCloseBtn')||e.target.closest('#csExit')){csCloseM();return;}
    if(e.target===ov)csCloseM();
  });
})();
function efaEcdHideSet(){const p=(typeof opsParts==='function')?opsParts(active):null;if(p&&p.base==='FCF'){if(p.mode==='FCL')return EFA_FCF_ECD_HIDE_FCL;if(p.mode==='LCL')return EFA_FCF_ECD_HIDE_LCL;return EFA_ECD_HIDE;}if(p&&p.mode==='LCL')return EFA_ECD_HIDE_LCL;if(p&&p.mode==='FCL')return EFA_ECD_HIDE_FCL;return EFA_ECD_HIDE;}
function efaHeaderName(c,raw){const p=(typeof opsParts==='function')?opsParts(active):null;if(!p)return raw;if(p.base==='EFA'){if(p.mode==='LCL'&&p.dept==='ECD'){if(c===11)return 'CARRIER';if(c===21)return 'CFS CUTOFF DATE';}if(p.mode==='FCL'&&p.dept==='ECD'){if(c===32)return 'SI READINESS';}}else if(p.base==='FCF'){if(p.dept==='ECD'){if(c===32)return 'SI READINESS';if(p.mode==='LCL'&&c===11)return 'CARRIER';}}else if(p.base==='DO'){if(p.dept==='ICD'){if(p.mode==='FCL'&&c===19)return 'CONTAINER/SEAL NO#';if(p.mode==='LCL'&&c===10)return 'CARRIER';}else if(p.dept==='FCD'){if(p.mode==='LCL'&&c===10)return 'CARRIER';}}return raw;}
const EFA_NOFUTURE=new Set([33,37,41,43,44]);
const CCD_NOFUTURE=new Set([33,36]);
const DCD_NOFUTURE=new Set([27,28]);
function efaNoFutureCols(){if(typeof efaFull==='function'&&efaFull())return EFA_NOFUTURE;if(typeof isCCDSheet==='function'&&isCCDSheet())return CCD_NOFUTURE;if(typeof isDCDSheet==='function'&&isDCDSheet())return DCD_NOFUTURE;return null;}
function efaHiddenCol(c){if(typeof efaFull==='function'&&efaFull())return efaEcdHideSet().has(c);if(typeof isCCDSheet==='function'&&isCCDSheet()){const p=opsParts(active);if(p&&p.mode==='FCL')return EFA_CCD_HIDE_FCL.has(c);}const _pd=opsParts(active);if(_pd&&_pd.base==='DO'&&_pd.dept==='ICD')return (_pd.mode==='LCL')?DO_ICD_HIDE_LCL.has(c):DO_ICD_HIDE_FCL.has(c);if(_pd&&_pd.base==='DO'&&_pd.dept==='FCD')return (_pd.mode==='LCL')?DO_FCD_HIDE_LCL.has(c):false;return false;}
function efaFull(){const p=opsParts(active);return !!p&&isOpsStyleBase(p.base)&&p.dept==='ECD';}
function efaSrcCol(c){const p=opsParts(active);if(!p||!isOpsStyleBase(p.base))return c;if(p.base==='EFA'){if(p.dept!=='CCD'&&p.dept!=='DCD'&&p.dept!=='FCD')return c;return (c<EFA_CCD_SRC.length)?EFA_CCD_SRC[c]:-1;}const map=opsSrcMap(p.base,p.dept);if(!map)return c;return (c<map.length)?map[c]:-1;}
function efaLeafSrcLen(){const p=opsParts(active);if(!p||!isOpsStyleBase(p.base))return 0;if(p.base==='EFA'){if(p.dept==='FCD')return EFA_FCD_SRC.length;if(p.dept==='CCD'||p.dept==='DCD')return EFA_CCD_SRC.length;return 0;}const map=opsSrcMap(p.base,p.dept);return map?map.length:0;}
let LOCKCOL=(function(){try{const d=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('shoptrans_lockcol_v1'));return (d&&typeof d==='object')?d:{};}catch(e){return{};}})();
function saveLockCol(){try{localStorage.setItem('shoptrans_lockcol_v1',JSON.stringify(LOCKCOL));}catch(e){}}
function colLocked(sheet,c){const a=LOCKCOL[sheet];return Array.isArray(a)&&a.indexOf(c)>=0;}
function setColLock(sheet,c,on){let a=(LOCKCOL[sheet]||[]).filter(x=>x!==c);if(on)a.push(c);LOCKCOL[sheet]=a;saveLockCol();}
function efaLockedCol(c){return colLocked(active,c);}
const KEY_EFA_RK='shoptrans_efa_rk_v1';
let EFA_RK_CTR=(function(){try{const v=parseInt(localStorage.getItem(KEY_EFA_RK),10);if(v>0)return v}catch(e){}return 0;})();
function efaNewRK(){EFA_RK_CTR++;try{localStorage.setItem(KEY_EFA_RK,String(EFA_RK_CTR))}catch(e){}return 'R'+EFA_RK_CTR;}
function fcfPush(srcSheet,r,toDept){
  const p=opsParts(srcSheet);
  if(!p||p.base!=='FCF'||opsStatusOf(srcSheet)!=='active')return;
  if(!((p.dept==='ECD'&&toDept==='DCD')||(p.dept==='DCD'&&toDept==='FCD')))return;
  const src=DATA[srcSheet]&&DATA[srcSheet][r];if(!src)return;
  const toKey=opsKeyOf({base:'FCF',mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:toDept});
  if(!DATA[toKey])DATA[toKey]=[opsLeafHeader('FCF',toDept).slice()];
  const srcKey=efaEnsureKey(srcSheet,r);
  const map=opsSrcMap('FCF',toDept),N=map.length;
  const srcIds=[13,14,15,16],toIds=srcIds.map(c=>map.indexOf(c));
  const norm=v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim().toUpperCase();
  const skVals=srcIds.map(c=>norm(src[c]));
  const hasId=skVals.some(v=>v!=='');const sk=skVals.join('|');
  const keyf=row=>toIds.map(c=>norm(row[c])).join('|');let target=-1;
  for(let i=1;i<DATA[toKey].length;i++){const row=DATA[toKey][i];
    if(hasId){if(keyf(row)===sk){target=i;break;}}
    else{let same=true;for(let j=0;j<N;j++){if(norm(row[j])!==norm(src[map[j]])){same=false;break;}}if(same){target=i;break;}}}
  if(srcKey){for(let i=1;i<DATA[toKey].length;i++){if(efaRowKey(toKey,DATA[toKey][i])===srcKey){target=i;break;}}}
  const hdrLen=((DATA[toKey][0])||[]).length||N;
  if(target<0){const nr=new Array(hdrLen).fill('');for(let j=0;j<N;j++)nr[j]=src[map[j]]==null?'':src[map[j]];nr[efaVisCols(toKey)]=srcKey;DATA[toKey].push(nr);}
  else{for(let j=0;j<N;j++)DATA[toKey][target][j]=src[map[j]]==null?'':src[map[j]];DATA[toKey][target][efaVisCols(toKey)]=srcKey;}
  save();
  if(typeof toast==='function')toast(target<0?('Sent to '+toDept):(toDept+' updated'));
  if(active===toKey)renderSheet();
}
function fcfUntick(srcSheet,r,toDept){
  const p=opsParts(srcSheet);
  if(!p||p.base!=='FCF'||opsStatusOf(srcSheet)!=='active')return true;
  const src=DATA[srcSheet]&&DATA[srcSheet][r];if(!src)return true;
  const toKey=opsKeyOf({base:'FCF',mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:toDept});if(!DATA[toKey])return true;
  const map=opsSrcMap('FCF',toDept);
  const srcIds=[13,14,15,16],toIds=srcIds.map(c=>map.indexOf(c));
  const norm=v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim().toUpperCase();
  const skVals=srcIds.map(c=>norm(src[c]));
  const hasId=skVals.some(v=>v!=='');const sk=skVals.join('|');
  const keyf=row=>toIds.map(c=>norm(row[c])).join('|');let target=-1;
  for(let i=1;i<DATA[toKey].length;i++){const row=DATA[toKey][i];
    if(hasId){if(keyf(row)===sk){target=i;break;}}
    else{let same=true;for(let j=0;j<map.length;j++){if(norm(row[j])!==norm(src[map[j]])){same=false;break;}}if(same){target=i;break;}}}
  if(target<0)return true;
  const row=DATA[toKey][target];
  for(let c=map.length;c<row.length;c++){const v=String(row[c]==null?'':row[c]).trim();if(v!==''&&v!=='FALSE')return false;}
  DATA[toKey].splice(target,1);save();
  if(active===toKey)renderSheet();
  return true;
}
function efaVisCols(sheet){const p=opsParts(sheet);if(!p||!isOpsStyleBase(p.base)||isFlatSheet(sheet))return 0;try{const h=opsLeafHeader(p.base,p.dept);return Array.isArray(h)?h.length:0;}catch(e){return 0;}}
function efaNCols(sheet){const n=ncols(sheet);const vc=efaVisCols(sheet);return (vc>0&&vc<n)?vc:n;}
function efaRowKey(sheet,row){const vc=efaVisCols(sheet);if(vc<=0||!row)return '';const k=row[vc];return (k==null)?'':String(k).trim();}
function efaEnsureKey(sheet,r){const vc=efaVisCols(sheet);const row=DATA[sheet]&&DATA[sheet][r];if(vc<=0||!row)return '';let k=row[vc];if(k==null||String(k).trim()===''){k=efaNewRK();row[vc]=k;}return String(k).trim();}
function efaParseADList(v){try{const o=JSON.parse(v);if(Array.isArray(o))return o;if(o&&typeof o==='object')return [o];}catch(_){}return [];}
function efaDaysAfter(ri){const row=DATA[active][ri]||[];const ata=parseDate(String(row[EFA_ATA_COL]||''));if(!ata)return '';const end=parseDate(String(row[EFA_PICKUP_COL]||''))||new Date();const a=Date.UTC(ata.getFullYear(),ata.getMonth(),ata.getDate());const b=Date.UTC(end.getFullYear(),end.getMonth(),end.getDate());return Math.floor((b-a)/86400000);}
function efaAutoFit(){
  if(!isEFASheet())return;
  const n=ncols(active);if(!COLW[active])COLW[active]=[];
  for(let c=0;c<n;c++){
    let w;
    if(c===EFA_AD_COL&&efaFull()){const hl=String((DATA[active][0]&&DATA[active][0][c])||'').length;w=Math.max(96,hl*7.2+30);}
    else w=autoWidth(active,c);
    if(EFA_DD[c]&&efaFull())w+=20;
    COLW[active][c]=Math.round(w);
  }
}
function efaUntickBCSent(srcSheet,r){
  const p=opsParts(srcSheet);
  if(!p||p.base!=='EFA'||p.dept!=='ECD'||opsStatusOf(srcSheet)!=='active')return true;
  const src=DATA[srcSheet]&&DATA[srcSheet][r];if(!src)return true;
  const ccd=opsKeyOf({base:'EFA',mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:'CCD'});if(!DATA[ccd])return true;
  const map=EFA_CCD_SRC;
  const srcIds=[13,14,15,16],ccdIds=srcIds.map(c=>map.indexOf(c));
  const norm=v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim().toUpperCase();
  const skVals=srcIds.map(c=>norm(src[c]));
  const hasId=skVals.some(v=>v!=='');const sk=skVals.join('|');
  const ccdKey=row=>ccdIds.map(c=>norm(row[c])).join('|');let target=-1;
  for(let i=1;i<DATA[ccd].length;i++){const row=DATA[ccd][i];
    if(hasId){if(ccdKey(row)===sk){target=i;break;}}
    else{let same=true;for(let j=0;j<map.length;j++){if(norm(row[j])!==norm(src[map[j]])){same=false;break;}}if(same){target=i;break;}}}
  if(target<0)return true;
  const row=DATA[ccd][target];
  for(let c=map.length;c<row.length;c++){const v=String(row[c]==null?'':row[c]).trim();if(v!==''&&v!=='FALSE')return false;}
  DATA[ccd].splice(target,1);save();
  if(active===ccd)renderSheet();
  return true;
}
function efaPushToCCD(srcSheet,r){
  const p=opsParts(srcSheet);
  if(!p||p.base!=='EFA'||p.dept!=='ECD'||opsStatusOf(srcSheet)!=='active')return;
  const src=DATA[srcSheet]&&DATA[srcSheet][r];if(!src)return;
  const ccd=opsKeyOf({base:'EFA',mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:'CCD'});
  if(!DATA[ccd])DATA[ccd]=[opsLeafHeader('EFA','CCD').slice()];
  const srcKey=efaEnsureKey(srcSheet,r);
  const map=EFA_CCD_SRC,N=map.length;
  const srcIds=[13,14,15,16],ccdIds=srcIds.map(c=>map.indexOf(c));
  const norm=v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim().toUpperCase();
  const skVals=srcIds.map(c=>norm(src[c]));
  const hasId=skVals.some(v=>v!=='');const sk=skVals.join('|');
  const ccdKey=row=>ccdIds.map(c=>norm(row[c])).join('|');let target=-1;
  for(let i=1;i<DATA[ccd].length;i++){const row=DATA[ccd][i];
    if(hasId){if(ccdKey(row)===sk){target=i;break;}}
    else{let same=true;for(let j=0;j<N;j++){if(norm(row[j])!==norm(src[map[j]])){same=false;break;}}if(same){target=i;break;}}}
  if(srcKey){for(let i=1;i<DATA[ccd].length;i++){if(efaRowKey(ccd,DATA[ccd][i])===srcKey){target=i;break;}}}
  const hdrLen=((DATA[ccd][0])||[]).length||N;
  if(target<0){const nr=new Array(hdrLen).fill('');for(let j=0;j<N;j++)nr[j]=src[map[j]]==null?'':src[map[j]];nr[efaVisCols(ccd)]=srcKey;DATA[ccd].push(nr);}
  else{for(let j=0;j<N;j++)DATA[ccd][target][j]=src[map[j]]==null?'':src[map[j]];DATA[ccd][target][efaVisCols(ccd)]=srcKey;}
  save();
  if(typeof toast==='function')toast(target<0?'Sent to CCD':'CCD updated');
  if(active===ccd)renderSheet();
}
function efaUntickStuffing(srcSheet,r){
  const p=opsParts(srcSheet);
  if(!p||p.base!=='EFA'||p.dept!=='CCD'||opsStatusOf(srcSheet)!=='active')return true;
  const src=DATA[srcSheet]&&DATA[srcSheet][r];if(!src)return true;
  const dcd=opsKeyOf({base:'EFA',mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:'DCD'});if(!DATA[dcd])return true;
  const N=EFA_DCD_SRC.length,ids=[12,13,14,15];
  const norm=v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim().toUpperCase();
  const skVals=ids.map(c=>norm(src[c]));
  const hasId=skVals.some(v=>v!=='');const sk=skVals.join('|');
  const keyf=row=>ids.map(c=>norm(row[c])).join('|');let target=-1;
  for(let i=1;i<DATA[dcd].length;i++){const row=DATA[dcd][i];
    if(hasId){if(keyf(row)===sk){target=i;break;}}
    else{let same=true;for(let j=0;j<N;j++){if(norm(row[j])!==norm(src[j])){same=false;break;}}if(same){target=i;break;}}}
  if(target<0)return true;
  const row=DATA[dcd][target];
  for(let c=N;c<row.length;c++){const v=String(row[c]==null?'':row[c]).trim();if(v!==''&&v!=='FALSE')return false;}
  DATA[dcd].splice(target,1);save();
  if(active===dcd)renderSheet();
  return true;
}

  for(let i=1;i<DATA[dcd].length;i++){const row=DATA[dcd][i];
