function efaSyncFromActiveECD(){
  const p=opsParts(active);
  if(!p||!isOpsStyleBase(p.base)||p.dept!=='ECD'||opsStatusOf(active)!=='active'||isFlatSheet(active))return;
  const erows=DATA[active];if(!erows||erows.length<2)return;
  const evc=efaVisCols(active);
  const norm=v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim().toUpperCase();
  const eIds=[13,14,15,16];
  const byKey={},byId={};
  for(let i=1;i<erows.length;i++){const k=erows[i][evc];if(k!=null&&String(k).trim()!=='')byKey[String(k).trim()]=i;const ks=eIds.map(c=>norm(erows[i][c]));if(ks.some(v=>v!==''))byId[ks.join('|')]=i;}
  opsDownDepts(p.base).forEach(dept=>{
    const leaf=opsKeyOf({base:p.base,mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:dept});const drows=DATA[leaf];if(!drows||drows.length<2)return;
    const dvc=efaVisCols(leaf);const map=opsSrcMap(p.base,dept);if(!map)return;const N=map.length,dIds=eIds.map(ec=>map.indexOf(ec));
    for(let i=1;i<drows.length;i++){const row=drows[i];let ei=null;const dk=row[dvc];
      if(dk!=null&&String(dk).trim()!=='')ei=byKey[String(dk).trim()];
      if(ei==null){const idk=dIds.map(c=>norm(row[c]));if(idk.some(v=>v!=='')){ei=byId[idk.join('|')];if(ei!=null){let k=erows[ei][evc];if(k==null||String(k).trim()===''){k=efaNewRK();erows[ei][evc]=k;}row[dvc]=String(k).trim();}}}
      if(ei==null)continue;
      const er=erows[ei];for(let j=0;j<N;j++){const nv=er[map[j]]==null?'':er[map[j]];if(String(row[j]==null?'':row[j])!==String(nv))row[j]=nv;}if(p.base==='EFA'){if(dept==='CCD'){const pv=er[33]==null?'':er[33];if(String(row[31]==null?'':row[31])!==String(pv))row[31]=pv;}else if(dept==='DCD'){const pv=er[37]==null?'':er[37];if(String(row[29]==null?'':row[29])!==String(pv))row[29]=pv;}}}
  });
}
function efaRefreshActiveDownstream(){
  const p=opsParts(active);
  if(!p||!isOpsStyleBase(p.base)||opsStatusOf(active)!=='active')return;
  const map=opsSrcMap(p.base,p.dept);if(!map)return;
  const ecd=opsKeyOf({base:p.base,mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:'ECD'});
  const erows=DATA[ecd],drows=DATA[active];
  if(!erows||erows.length<2||!drows||drows.length<2)return;
  const evc=efaVisCols(ecd),dvc=efaVisCols(active);
  const N=map.length,eIds=[13,14,15,16],dIds=eIds.map(ec=>map.indexOf(ec));
  const norm=v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim().toUpperCase();
  const byKey={},byId={};
  for(let i=1;i<erows.length;i++){const k=erows[i][evc];if(k!=null&&String(k).trim()!=='')byKey[String(k).trim()]=i;const ks=eIds.map(c=>norm(erows[i][c]));if(ks.some(v=>v!==''))byId[ks.join('|')]=i;}
  let changed=false;
  for(let i=1;i<drows.length;i++){const row=drows[i];let ei=null;const dk=row[dvc];
    if(dk!=null&&String(dk).trim()!=='')ei=byKey[String(dk).trim()];
    if(ei==null){const idk=dIds.map(c=>norm(row[c]));if(idk.some(v=>v!=='')){ei=byId[idk.join('|')];if(ei!=null){let k=erows[ei][evc];if(k==null||String(k).trim()===''){k=efaNewRK();erows[ei][evc]=k;}row[dvc]=String(k).trim();changed=true;}}}
    if(ei==null)continue;
    const er=erows[ei];for(let j=0;j<N;j++){const nv=er[map[j]]==null?'':er[map[j]];if(String(row[j]==null?'':row[j])!==String(nv)){row[j]=nv;changed=true;}}if(p.base==='EFA'){if(p.dept==='CCD'){const pv=er[33]==null?'':er[33];if(String(row[31]==null?'':row[31])!==String(pv)){row[31]=pv;changed=true;}}else if(p.dept==='DCD'){const pv=er[37]==null?'':er[37];if(String(row[29]==null?'':row[29])!==String(pv)){row[29]=pv;changed=true;}}}}
  if(changed)save();
}
function efaCellHTML(ri,c,val,f,span,st){
  if(EFA_DD[c]&&efaFull()){const _sv=String(val||'').trim();const _o=_sv?ddOptions(c).find(x=>x.toUpperCase()===_sv.toUpperCase()):'';return '<td class="cell ddcell" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'"'+span+(st?' style="'+st+'"':'')+'><span class="ddval">'+esc(_o||'')+'</span><span class="ddcaret" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'">\u25be</span></td>';}
  if(c===EFA_AD_COL&&efaFull()){const has=String(val||'').trim()!=='';return '<td class="cell adcell" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'" data-view="1"'+span+(st?' style="'+st+'"':'')+'><button class="ad-btn'+(has?' has':'')+'" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'">Detail</button></td>';}
  if(c===EFA_AQ_COL&&efaFull()){const d=efaDaysAfter(ri);return '<td class="cell aqcell" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'"'+span+(st?' style="'+st+'"':'')+'>'+(d===''?'':esc(String(d)))+'</td>';}
  return null;
}
function ccdCellHTML(ri,c,val,f,span,st){
  if(CCD_DD[c]){const _sv=String(val||'').trim();const _o=_sv?ddOptions(c).find(x=>x.toUpperCase()===_sv.toUpperCase()):'';return '<td class="cell ddcell" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'"'+span+(st?' style="'+st+'"':'')+'><span class="ddval">'+esc(_o||'')+'</span><span class="ddcaret" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'">\u25be</span></td>';}
  if(c===CCD_TRUCK_COL){const has=tkParse(val).length>0;return '<td class="cell tkcell" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'"'+span+(st?' style="'+st+'"':'')+'><button class="ad-btn'+(has?' has':'')+'" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'">'+(has?'Detail':'Add')+'</button></td>';}
  if(c===CCD_PICKUP_COL){const has=efaParseADList(val).length>0;return '<td class="cell adcell" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'"'+span+(st?' style="'+st+'"':'')+'><button class="ad-btn'+(has?' has':'')+'" data-r="'+ri+'" data-c="'+c+'">'+(has?'Detail':'Add')+'</button></td>';}
  return null;
}
/* dropdown popup */
let ddCell=null,ddMode='pick',ddFilter='';
function openDD(cell,seed){const ed=$('#ssheet').querySelector('td.cell[contenteditable="true"]');if(ed)ed.blur();ddCell=cell;ddMode='pick';ddFilter=seed||'';const r=+cell.dataset.r,c=+cell.dataset.c;lastRC={r,c};renderDD();$('#ddpop').style.display='block';positionDD();const si=$('#ddpop').querySelector('.ddsearch');if(si)setTimeout(()=>{si.focus();si.setSelectionRange(si.value.length,si.value.length);},10);}
function positionDD(){if(!ddCell)return;const rc=ddCell.getBoundingClientRect();const dp=$('#ddpop');const ww=Math.max(48,Math.round(rc.width));dp.style.width=ww+'px';dp.style.left=Math.max(8,Math.min(rc.left,window.innerWidth-ww-8))+'px';dp.style.top=Math.min(rc.bottom+4,window.innerHeight-Math.min(360,dp.offsetHeight+20))+'px';}
function ddRowsHTML(){const c=+ddCell.dataset.c,r=+ddCell.dataset.r;const cur=String(DATA[active][r][c]||'');const f=ddFilter.trim().toUpperCase();const shown=ddOptions(c).filter(o=>!f||o.toUpperCase().indexOf(f)>=0);if(!shown.length)return '<div class="ddnone">No match</div>';return shown.map(o=>'<div class="ddrow"><button class="ddopt'+(o===cur?' sel':'')+'" data-v="'+esc(o)+'">'+esc(o)+'</button>'+(ddMode==='edit'?'<button class="ddx" data-rm="'+esc(o)+'" title="Remove">\u00d7</button>':'')+'</div>').join('');}
function renderDD(){let h='<div class="ddsearchwrap"><input class="ddsearch" type="text" value="'+esc(ddFilter)+'"></div><div class="ddlist">'+ddRowsHTML()+'</div><div class="dderr" id="ddErr"></div>';if(ddMode==='pick'){h+='<button class="ddfoot ddedit">\u270e Edit</button>';}else{h+='<div class="ddaddwrap"><input class="ddadd" type="text" placeholder="New item"><button class="ddaddbtn" title="Add">\u2713</button></div><button class="ddfoot dddone">Done</button>';}$('#ddpop').innerHTML=h;}
function ddListOnly(){const ld=$('#ddpop').querySelector('.ddlist');if(ld)ld.innerHTML=ddRowsHTML();}
function ddSelect(v){const c=+ddCell.dataset.c,r=+ddCell.dataset.r;DATA[active][r][c]=v;save();closeDD();renderSheet();}
function ddErrMsg(m){const e=$('#ddErr');if(e)e.textContent=m||'';}
function closeDD(){$('#ddpop').style.display='none';ddCell=null;}
$('#ddpop').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(!ddCell)return;const c=+ddCell.dataset.c;
  if(e.target.closest('.ddedit')){ddMode='edit';renderDD();positionDD();return;}
  if(e.target.closest('.dddone')){ddMode='pick';renderDD();positionDD();return;}
  const x=e.target.closest('.ddx');if(x){const v=x.dataset.rm;if(ddInUse(c,v)){ddErrMsg('Item in use. Cannot remove.');}else{ddRemoveOption(c,v);ddErrMsg('');renderDD();positionDD();}return;}
  if(e.target.closest('.ddaddbtn')){const inp=$('#ddpop').querySelector('.ddadd');const v=(inp?inp.value:'').trim();if(!v)return;if(ddOptions(c).some(o=>o.toUpperCase()===v.toUpperCase())){ddErrMsg('Already exists.');return;}ddAddOption(c,v);ddErrMsg('');renderDD();positionDD();const ni=$('#ddpop').querySelector('.ddadd');if(ni)ni.focus();return;}
  const opt=e.target.closest('.ddopt');if(opt){ddSelect(opt.dataset.v);return;}
});
$('#ddpop').addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.target.classList.contains('ddsearch')){ddFilter=e.target.value;ddListOnly();}});
$('#ddpop').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(e.target.classList.contains('ddsearch')){if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();const c=+ddCell.dataset.c;const f=ddFilter.trim().toUpperCase();const shown=ddOptions(c).filter(o=>!f||o.toUpperCase().indexOf(f)>=0);if(shown.length)ddSelect(shown[0]);return;}if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();closeDD();return;}}
  if(e.target.classList.contains('ddadd')&&e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();const ab=$('#ddpop').querySelector('.ddaddbtn');if(ab)ab.click();}
});
document.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{if($('#ddpop').style.display==='block'&&!e.target.closest('#ddpop')&&!e.target.closest('.ddcaret'))closeDD();});
/* pickup-info (AD) modal */
let adRow=null,adList=[],adSel=new Set(),adViewOnly=false;
function efaADSuggestions(key){const set=new Set();const rows=DATA[active]||[];for(let i=1;i<rows.length;i++){efaParseADList(rows[i][EFA_AD_COL]).forEach(o=>{if(o&&o[key]&&String(o[key]).trim())set.add(String(o[key]).trim());});}return Array.from(set);}
function adFillDatalists(){['driver','truck','trailer'].forEach(k=>{const dl=document.getElementById('adlist_'+k);if(dl)dl.innerHTML=efaADSuggestions(k).map(v=>'<option value="'+esc(v)+'"></option>').join('');});}
function adToday(){const d=new Date();const p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');return p(d.getDate())+'/'+p(d.getMonth()+1)+'/'+d.getFullYear();}function adSelable(){return adList.map((o,i)=>i).filter(i=>!adList[i].sent);}function updateAdSentBtns(){const total=adList.length;const sentCount=adList.filter(o=>o.sent).length;const selCount=adSel.size;const allOn=(total>0&&sentCount===0&&selCount===total&&selCount>0);const sentOn=(selCount>0&&!allOn);const ba=document.getElementById('adSentAll'),bs=document.getElementById('adSent');if(ba){ba.disabled=!allOn;ba.classList.toggle('on',allOn);}if(bs){bs.disabled=!sentOn;bs.classList.toggle('on',sentOn);}const all=document.getElementById('adAll');if(all){const sa=adSelable();all.checked=sa.length>0&&sa.every(i=>adSel.has(i));all.disabled=!sa.length;}}function adToggleAll(on){adSel.clear();if(on){adList.forEach((o,i)=>{if(!o.sent)adSel.add(i);});}document.querySelectorAll('#adTable .adck').forEach(cb=>{const i=+cb.dataset.i;if(!cb.disabled)cb.checked=adSel.has(i);});updateAdSentBtns();}function adDoSend(){if(adRow==null||!adSel.size)return;const today=adToday();let changed=false;adSel.forEach(i=>{if(adList[i]&&!adList[i].sent){adList[i].sent=today;changed=true;}});if(changed){DATA[active][adRow][adCol()]=JSON.stringify(adList);save();}adSel.clear();renderADTable();renderSheet();}function renderADTable(){const vo=adViewOnly;const _T=$('#adTable');if(_T){_T.classList.toggle('adview',!!vo);const _w=_T.closest('.adtablewrap');if(_w)_w.classList.toggle('advgap',!!vo);}if(vo){const cols=['CONTAINER NO#','SEAL NO#','DRIVER NAME','PHONE','DRIVER ID','TRUCK NO#','TRAILER NO#','NOTES'];let h='<thead><tr>'+cols.map(c=>'<th>'+c+'</th>').join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>';if(!adList.length){h+='<tr class="adempty"><td colspan="'+cols.length+'">No records yet</td></tr>';}else{adList.forEach(o=>{h+='<tr><td>'+esc(o.container||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.seal||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.driver||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.phone||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.id||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.truck||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.trailer||'')+'</td><td class="adnotes">'+esc(o.notes||'')+'</td></tr>';});}h+='</tbody>';$('#adTable').innerHTML=h;return;}const sa=adSelable();const allChecked=sa.length>0&&sa.every(i=>adSel.has(i));const cols=['CONTAINER NO#','SEAL NO#','DRIVER NAME','PHONE','DRIVER ID','TRUCK NO#','TRAILER NO#','NOTES','SENT DATE'];let h='<thead><tr><th class="adckcol"><label class="adallwrap"><input type="checkbox" id="adAll"'+(allChecked?' checked':'')+(sa.length?'':' disabled')+'><span>All</span></label></th><th>ORDER</th>'+cols.map(c=>'<th>'+c+'</th>').join('')+'<th></th></tr></thead><tbody>';if(!adList.length){h+='<tr class="adempty"><td colspan="12">No records yet</td></tr>';}else{adList.forEach((o,i)=>{const sent=o.sent?String(o.sent):'';const cls=sent?' class="adsent"':'';const ck='<input type="checkbox" class="adck" data-i="'+i+'"'+(adSel.has(i)?' checked':'')+(sent?' disabled':'')+'>';h+='<tr'+cls+'><td class="adckcol">'+ck+'</td><td>'+(i+1)+'</td><td>'+esc(o.container||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.seal||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.driver||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.phone||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.id||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.truck||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.trailer||'')+'</td><td class="adnotes">'+esc(o.notes||'')+'</td><td class="adsentdate">'+esc(sent)+'</td><td><button class="adrm" data-i="'+i+'" title="Remove">\u00d7</button></td></tr>';});}h+='</tbody>';$('#adTable').innerHTML=h;updateAdSentBtns();}
function ccdPickupFor(ecdSheet,r){const p=opsParts(ecdSheet);if(!p||p.base!=='EFA')return [];const ccd=opsKeyOf({base:'EFA',mode:p.mode,type:p.type,dept:'CCD'});const crows=DATA[ccd];if(!crows)return [];const src=DATA[ecdSheet]&&DATA[ecdSheet][r];if(!src)return [];const map=EFA_CCD_SRC;const srcIds=[13,14,15,16];const ccdIds=srcIds.map(c=>map.indexOf(c));const norm=v=>String(v==null?'':v).trim().toUpperCase();const skVals=srcIds.map(c=>norm(src[c]));const hasId=skVals.some(v=>v!=='');const sk=skVals.join('|');let target=-1;for(let i=1;i<crows.length;i++){const row=crows[i];if(hasId){if(ccdIds.map(c=>norm(row[c])).join('|')===sk){target=i;break;}}else{let same=true;for(let j=0;j<map.length;j++){if(norm(row[j])!==norm(src[map[j]])){same=false;break;}}if(same){target=i;break;}}}if(target<0)return [];return efaParseADList(crows[target][CCD_PICKUP_COL]);}
function openADModal(ri,viewOnly){adViewOnly=!!viewOnly;adRow=ri;adList=adViewOnly?ccdPickupFor(active,ri):efaParseADList(DATA[active][ri][adCol()]);adSel.clear();(function(){const m=document.getElementById('adMsg');if(m)m.textContent='';})();EFA_AD_FIELDS.forEach(k=>{const inp=document.getElementById('ad_'+k);if(inp)inp.value='';});['ad_container','ad_phone'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.classList.remove('err');});const ovl=$('#adOverlay');const form=ovl.querySelector('.efaform');if(form)form.style.display=adViewOnly?'none':'';const btns=ovl.querySelector('.efabtns');if(btns)btns.style.display=adViewOnly?'none':'';const ex=document.getElementById('adExit');if(ex)ex.style.display=adViewOnly?'':'none';const _isDupAd=!!(opsParts(active)&&opsParts(active).base==='DUP');const _adHd=ovl.querySelector('.efahead');if(_adHd&&_adHd.firstChild&&_adHd.firstChild.nodeType===3)_adHd.firstChild.nodeValue=_isDupAd?'Delivery information':'Pickup information';const sub=ovl.querySelector('.efahead .sub');if(sub)sub.textContent=adViewOnly?'recorded list (from CCD)':(_isDupAd?'delivery details':'Column AD \u00b7 pickup details');renderADTable();ovl.classList.add('on');if(!adViewOnly){adFillDatalists();setTimeout(()=>{const fi=document.getElementById('ad_container');if(fi)fi.focus();},30);}}
function closeADModal(){$('#adOverlay').classList.remove('on');adRow=null;adList=[];renderSheet();}
function adIsDup(o){const norm=s=>String(s==null?'':s).trim().toLowerCase();return adList.some(r=>EFA_AD_FIELDS.every(k=>norm(r[k])===norm(o[k])));}
function adAddRow(){if(adRow==null)return;const o={};let any=false;EFA_AD_FIELDS.forEach(k=>{const inp=document.getElementById('ad_'+k);const v=inp?inp.value.trim():'';o[k]=v;if(v)any=true;});if(!efaADValidate()){return;}if(o.container)o.container=o.container.toUpperCase();if(!any){return;}const _m=document.getElementById('adMsg');if(adIsDup(o)){if(_m)_m.textContent='Duplicate entry \u2014 this record already exists.';return;}if(_m)_m.textContent='';adList.push(o);DATA[active][adRow][adCol()]=JSON.stringify(adList);save();EFA_AD_FIELDS.forEach(k=>{const inp=document.getElementById('ad_'+k);if(inp)inp.value='';});adFillDatalists();renderADTable();renderSheet();const fi=document.getElementById('ad_container');if(fi)fi.focus();}
function adRemoveRow(i){if(adRow==null)return;adList.splice(i,1);adSel.clear();DATA[active][adRow][adCol()]=adList.length?JSON.stringify(adList):'';save();renderADTable();renderSheet();}
$('#adCancel').onclick=closeADModal;$('#adExit').onclick=closeADModal;
$('#adSave').onclick=adAddRow;
$('#adTable').addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('.adrm');if(b)adRemoveRow(+b.dataset.i);});
$('#adTable').addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.id==='adAll'){adToggleAll(e.target.checked);return;}const cb=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('.adck');if(cb){const i=+cb.dataset.i;if(cb.checked)adSel.add(i);else adSel.delete(i);updateAdSentBtns();}});

$('#adSent').onclick=()=>{if($('#adSent').disabled)return;confirmBox('Are you sure to send it out?',adDoSend,'YES','NO');};

const RE_CONT=/^[A-Za-z]{4}\d{7}$/;const RE_PHONE=/^(?=.*\d)[0-9()-]+$/;
function efaADValidate(){const cI=document.getElementById('ad_container'),pI=document.getElementById('ad_phone');let ok=true;if(cI){const v=cI.value.trim();const bad=v!==''&&!RE_CONT.test(v);cI.classList.toggle('err',bad);if(bad)ok=false;}if(pI){const v=pI.value.trim();const bad=v!==''&&!RE_PHONE.test(v);pI.classList.toggle('err',bad);if(bad)ok=false;}return ok;}
(function(){const p=document.getElementById('ad_phone');if(p)p.addEventListener('input',function(){this.value=this.value.replace(/[^0-9()-]/g,'');efaADValidate();});const c=document.getElementById('ad_container');if(c)c.addEventListener('input',efaADValidate);})();
$('#adOverlay').onclick=e=>{if(e.target===$('#adOverlay'))closeADModal();};
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if($('#adOverlay').classList.contains('on')&&e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();closeADModal();}});
let tkRow=null,tkList=[],tkSel=new Set();
const TK_FIELDS=['date','time','content'];
function tkParse(v){try{const o=JSON.parse(v);if(Array.isArray(o))return o;if(o&&typeof o==='object')return [o];}catch(_){}return [];}
function tkSelable(){return tkList.map((o,i)=>i).filter(i=>!tkList[i].sent);}
function updateTkSentBtns(){const selCount=tkSel.size;const sentOn=selCount>0;const bs=document.getElementById('tkSent');if(bs){bs.disabled=!sentOn;bs.classList.toggle('on',sentOn);}const all=document.getElementById('tkAll');if(all){const sa=tkSelable();all.checked=sa.length>0&&sa.every(i=>tkSel.has(i));all.disabled=!sa.length;}}
function tkToggleAll(on){tkSel.clear();if(on){tkList.forEach((o,i)=>{if(!o.sent)tkSel.add(i);});}document.querySelectorAll('#tkTable .adck').forEach(cb=>{const i=+cb.dataset.i;if(!cb.disabled)cb.checked=tkSel.has(i);});updateTkSentBtns();}
function tkDoSend(){if(tkRow==null||!tkSel.size)return;const today=adToday();let changed=false;tkSel.forEach(i=>{if(tkList[i]&&!tkList[i].sent){tkList[i].sent=today;changed=true;}});if(changed){DATA[active][tkRow][tkCol()]=JSON.stringify(tkList);save();}tkSel.clear();renderTKTable();renderSheet();}
function renderTKTable(){const sa=tkSelable();const allChecked=sa.length>0&&sa.every(i=>tkSel.has(i));const cols=['PICKUP DATE','PICKUP TIME','CONTENT','SENT DATE'];let h='<thead><tr><th class="adckcol"><label class="adallwrap"><input type="checkbox" id="tkAll"'+(allChecked?' checked':'')+(sa.length?'':' disabled')+'><span>All</span></label></th><th>ORDER</th>'+cols.map(c=>'<th>'+c+'</th>').join('')+'<th></th></tr></thead><tbody>';if(!tkList.length){h+='<tr class="adempty"><td colspan="7">No records yet</td></tr>';}else{tkList.forEach((o,i)=>{const sent=o.sent?String(o.sent):'';const cls=sent?' class="adsent"':'';const ck='<input type="checkbox" class="adck" data-i="'+i+'"'+(tkSel.has(i)?' checked':'')+(sent?' disabled':'')+'>';h+='<tr'+cls+'><td class="adckcol">'+ck+'</td><td>'+(i+1)+'</td><td>'+esc(o.date||'')+'</td><td>'+esc(o.time||'')+'</td><td class="adnotes">'+esc(o.content||'')+'</td><td class="adsentdate">'+esc(sent)+'</td><td><button class="adrm" data-i="'+i+'" title="Remove">\u00d7</button></td></tr>';});}h+='</tbody>';$('#tkTable').innerHTML=h;updateTkSentBtns();}
function tkFillForm(){TK_FIELDS.forEach(k=>{const inp=document.getElementById('tk_'+k);if(inp)inp.value='';});}
function openTKModal(ri){tkRow=ri;tkList=tkParse(DATA[active][ri][tkCol()]);tkSel.clear();const m=document.getElementById('tkMsg');if(m)m.textContent='';tkFillForm();renderTKTable();$('#tkOverlay').classList.add('on');}
function closeTKModal(){closeTkDatePop();closeTkTimePop();$('#tkOverlay').classList.remove('on');tkRow=null;tkList=[];renderSheet();}
function tkIsDup(o){const norm=s=>String(s==null?'':s).trim().toLowerCase();return tkList.some(r=>TK_FIELDS.every(k=>norm(r[k])===norm(o[k])));}
function tkAddRow(){if(tkRow==null)return;const o={};let any=false;TK_FIELDS.forEach(k=>{const inp=document.getElementById('tk_'+k);const v=inp?inp.value.trim():'';o[k]=v;if(v)any=true;});const _m=document.getElementById('tkMsg');if(o.date&&!parseDate(o.date)){if(_m)_m.textContent='Invalid date (use DD/MM/YYYY).';return;}if(o.time&&!parseTime(o.time)){if(_m)_m.textContent='Invalid time (use HH:MM).';return;}if(!any)return;if(o.date){const d=parseDate(o.date);if(d)o.date=fmtDate(d);}if(o.time){const tm=parseTime(o.time);if(tm)o.time=fmtTime(tm);}if(tkIsDup(o)){if(_m)_m.textContent='Duplicate entry \u2014 this record already exists.';return;}if(_m)_m.textContent='';tkList.push(o);DATA[active][tkRow][tkCol()]=JSON.stringify(tkList);save();tkFillForm();renderTKTable();renderSheet();const fi=document.getElementById('tk_date');if(fi)fi.focus();}
function tkRemoveRow(i){if(tkRow==null)return;tkList.splice(i,1);tkSel.clear();DATA[active][tkRow][tkCol()]=tkList.length?JSON.stringify(tkList):'';save();renderTKTable();renderSheet();}

let tkDpView=new Date();
function openTkDatePop(){closeTkTimePop();const inp=document.getElementById('tk_date');if(!inp)return;tkDpView=parseDate(inp.value)||new Date();renderTkDatePop();const dp=$('#tkDatePop');dp.style.display='block';const rc=inp.getBoundingClientRect();dp.style.left=Math.max(8,Math.min(rc.left,window.innerWidth-250))+'px';dp.style.top=Math.min(rc.bottom+4,window.innerHeight-320)+'px';}
function closeTkDatePop(){const dp=document.getElementById('tkDatePop');if(dp)dp.style.display='none';}
function renderTkDatePop(){const y=tkDpView.getFullYear(),m=tkDpView.getMonth();const MM=['January','February','March','April','May','June','July','August','September','October','November','December'];const first=new Date(y,m,1).getDay(),days=new Date(y,m+1,0).getDate();const today=new Date();const cur=parseDate((document.getElementById('tk_date')||{}).value||'');let h='<div class="dp-head"><button class="dp-nav" data-tkdp="prev">\u2039</button><span class="dp-title">'+MM[m]+' '+y+'</span><button class="dp-nav" data-tkdp="next">\u203a</button></div><div class="dp-grid">';['Su','Mo','Tu','We','Th','Fr','Sa'].forEach(d=>h+='<span class="dp-w">'+d+'</span>');for(let i=0;i<first;i++)h+='<span></span>';for(let d=1;d<=days;d++){const isT=today.getFullYear()===y&&today.getMonth()===m&&today.getDate()===d;const isS=cur&&cur.getFullYear()===y&&cur.getMonth()===m&&cur.getDate()===d;h+='<button class="dp-d '+(isS?'sel':'')+' '+(isT?'today':'')+'" data-tkday="'+d+'">'+d+'</button>';}h+='</div><div class="dp-foot"><button class="dp-clear" data-tkdp="clear">Clear</button><button class="dp-today" data-tkdp="now">Today</button></div>';$('#tkDatePop').innerHTML=h;}
function tkSetDate(dt){const inp=document.getElementById('tk_date');if(inp)inp.value=dt?fmtDate(dt):'';const m=document.getElementById('tkMsg');if(m)m.textContent='';closeTkDatePop();}
$('#tkDatePop').addEventListener('click',e=>{const nav=e.target.closest('[data-tkdp]');if(nav){const a=nav.dataset.tkdp;if(a==='prev'){tkDpView=new Date(tkDpView.getFullYear(),tkDpView.getMonth()-1,1);renderTkDatePop();return}if(a==='next'){tkDpView=new Date(tkDpView.getFullYear(),tkDpView.getMonth()+1,1);renderTkDatePop();return}if(a==='clear'){tkSetDate(null);return}if(a==='now'){tkSetDate(new Date());return}}const d=e.target.closest('[data-tkday]');if(d)tkSetDate(new Date(tkDpView.getFullYear(),tkDpView.getMonth(),+d.dataset.tkday));});
function openTkTimePop(){closeTkDatePop();const inp=document.getElementById('tk_time');if(!inp)return;renderTkTimePop();const tp=$('#tkTimePop');tp.style.display='block';const rc=inp.getBoundingClientRect();tp.style.left=Math.max(8,Math.min(rc.left,window.innerWidth-140))+'px';tp.style.top=Math.min(rc.bottom+4,window.innerHeight-260)+'px';const s=tp.querySelector('.tkt-sel');if(s)s.scrollIntoView({block:'center'});}
function closeTkTimePop(){const tp=document.getElementById('tkTimePop');if(tp)tp.style.display='none';}
function renderTkTimePop(){const cur=parseTime((document.getElementById('tk_time')||{}).value||'');let h='<div class="tkt-list">';for(let hh=0;hh<24;hh++){for(let mm=0;mm<60;mm+=30){const lab=String(hh).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(mm).padStart(2,'0');const isS=cur&&cur.h===hh&&cur.m===mm;h+='<button class="tkt-opt'+(isS?' tkt-sel':'')+'" data-tkt="'+lab+'">'+lab+'</button>';}}h+='</div>';$('#tkTimePop').innerHTML=h;}

document.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{const dp=document.getElementById('tkDatePop');if(dp&&dp.style.display==='block'&&!e.target.closest('#tkDatePop')&&e.target.id!=='tk_date')closeTkDatePop();const tp=document.getElementById('tkTimePop');if(tp&&tp.style.display==='block'&&!e.target.closest('#tkTimePop')&&e.target.id!=='tk_time')closeTkTimePop();});
function efaRenderEmptyRight(){
  RENDER_LAST_SHEET=active;
  const _tb=document.querySelector('.main-head .toolbar');if(_tb)_tb.style.display='none';
  const sn=$('#sheetName');if(sn)sn.textContent='';
  const ot=$('#opsTabs');if(ot){ot.style.display='none';ot.innerHTML='';}
  const ct=$('#checklistTabs');if(ct){ct.style.display='none';ct.innerHTML='';}
  const sw=$('#sheetwrap');if(sw)sw.style.display='none';
  let ph=$('#opsEmpty');
  if(!ph){ph=document.createElement('div');ph.id='opsEmpty';const m=document.querySelector('.main');if(m)m.appendChild(ph);}
  ph.style.display='flex';
  ph.innerHTML='<div class="ops-empty-card">Select <b>FCL</b>, <b>LCL</b> or <b>AIR</b> in the sidebar to open a sheet.</div>';
}
function renderSheet(){
  if(isOpsBase(active)){efaRenderEmptyRight();return;}
  {const _phE=$('#opsEmpty');if(_phE)_phE.style.display='none';const _swE=$('#sheetwrap');if(_swE)_swE.style.display='';const _tbS=document.querySelector('.main-head .toolbar');if(_tbS)_tbS.style.display='';}
  if(active!==RENDER_LAST_SHEET)efaRefreshActiveDownstream();
  const _ssEl=$('#ssheet');const _keepScroll=(active===RENDER_LAST_SHEET&&_ssEl);
  const _sp=SCROLLPOS[active]||{l:0,t:0};const _keepL=_keepScroll?_ssEl.scrollLeft:_sp.l,_keepT=_keepScroll?_ssEl.scrollTop:_sp.t;RENDER_LAST_SHEET=active;
  $('#sheetName').textContent=isOpsSheet(active)?'':opsSheetLabel(active);
  renderChecklistTabs();renderOpsTabs();
  const M=document.querySelector('.main');
  M.classList.remove('regs');
  if(active==='VBPL'){M.classList.remove('sales','docs');M.classList.add('regs');renderRegs();return;}
  if(CHECKLIST_SET.has(active)){M.classList.remove('sales');M.classList.add('docs');CKL_FILTER='all';CKL_Q='';renderDocsChecklist();return;}
  M.classList.remove('docs');
  if(isOpsSheet(active)){ensureTS(active);const _tp=tsPhys(active);if(_tp>=0){const _d=DATA[active];let _ch=false;for(let _i=1;_i<_d.length;_i++){const _r=_d[_i];if(!_r)continue;const _has=_r.some((v,j)=>j!==_tp&&String(v==null?'':v).trim()!=='');if(_has){while(_r.length<=_tp)_r.push('');if(!String(_r[_tp]==null?'':_r[_tp]).trim()){_r[_tp]=nowTS();_ch=true;}}}if(_ch&&typeof save==='function')save();}}
  const n=(function(){let _n=normalize(active);const _vc=efaVisCols(active);if(_vc>0&&_vc<_n)_n=_vc;return _n;})();
  const ORD=buildColOrd(active,n);const USERSET=opsUserSet(active);const _maxPhys=ORD.length?Math.max(n,Math.max.apply(null,ORD)+1):n;
  efaAutoFit();
  const W=widths(active,_maxPhys);
  let totalW=0;ORD.forEach(c=>{totalW+=(W[c]||DEFAULT_CW);});
  const idx=filteredRows();
  const DCOL=isOpsSheet(active)&&!isFlatSheet(active)?doneCol(active):-1;
  const isEFAbase=isOpsSheet(active)&&isOpsStyleBase(opsBaseOf(active))&&!isFlatSheet(active);
  const shown=idx.slice(0,Math.max(visible,1));
  _lastORD=ORD;_lastShown=shown;
  let cols='';ORD.forEach(c=>{cols+=`<col style="width:${W[c]||DEFAULT_CW}px">`;});
  // frozen column-letter bar
  let head='<tr>';let _vh=0;
  ORD.forEach(c=>{head+=`<th data-col="${c}">${colLetter(_vh)}<span class="rsz" data-rsz="${c}"></span></th>`;_vh++;});
  head+='</tr>';
  $('#colbar').innerHTML=`<table style="width:${totalW}px"><colgroup>${cols}</colgroup><thead>${head}</thead></table>`;
  // frozen row-number bar + data body
  const shownSet=new Set(shown);
  const validMerges=query?[]:(MERGES[active]||[]).filter(m=>{for(let r=m[0];r<=m[2];r++)if(!shownSet.has(r))return false;return true;});
  const regAt=(r,c)=>{for(const m of validMerges){if(r>=m[0]&&r<=m[2]&&c>=m[1]&&c<=m[3])return m}return null};
  let rb='',body='';
  for(const ri of shown){
    const r=DATA[active][ri];const isHdr=ri===0;
    const rh=(ROWH[active]&&ROWH[active][ri]!=null)?ROWH[active][ri]:null;
    rb+=`<tr><td class="rownum ${isHdr?'hdrn':''}"${isHdr?'':` data-row="${ri}"`}>${ri+1}<span class="rrz" data-rrz="${ri}" title="Resize row"></span></td></tr>`;
    body+=`<tr class="${isHdr?'hdr':('zeb'+(ri%2))}">`;
    for(const c of ORD){
      const reg=regAt(ri,c);
      if(reg&&!(ri===reg[0]&&c===reg[1]))continue;
      let span='';if(reg){const rs=reg[2]-reg[0]+1,cs=reg[3]-reg[1]+1;if(rs>1)span+=` rowspan="${rs}"`;if(cs>1)span+=` colspan="${cs}"`;}
      let val=r[c];if(isHdr&&typeof efaHeaderName==='function')val=efaHeaderName(c,val);const f=fmtOf(ri,c);
      let st='';if(rh)st+=`height:${rh}px;max-height:${rh}px;overflow:hidden;`;
      if(f&&f.color)st+='color:'+f.color+';';
      if(f&&f.bg)st+='background:'+f.bg+';';
      if(!isHdr){const _uc=ucolFmt(active,c);if(_uc){if(_uc.kind==='checkbox'){const _on=(String(r[c]).toUpperCase()==='TRUE');body+=`<td class="cell bool${c===xferCol(active)?' xfertrig':''}" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}"${span}${st?` style="${st}"`:''}><input type="checkbox" class="bchk" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}" ${_on?'checked':''}></td>`;continue;}if(_uc.kind==='dropdown'){const _cv=r[c]==null?'':String(r[c]);body+=`<td class="cell ddcell" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}"${span}${st?` style="${st}"`:''}><span class="ddval">${esc(_cv)}</span><span class="ddcaret" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}">\u25be</span></td>`;continue;}}}
      if(USERSET.has(c)){body+=`<td class="cell" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}"${span}${st?` style="${st}"`:''}>${esc(r[c]==null?'':String(r[c]))}</td>`;continue;}
      const isDoneCell=(DCOL>=0&&!isHdr&&c===DCOL);
      const efaSp=(isEFAbase&&!isHdr)?(isCCDSheet()?ccdCellHTML(ri,c,val,f,span,st):efaCellHTML(ri,c,val,f,span,st)):null;
      if(efaSp!==null){body+=efaSp;}
      else if(!isHdr&&isCsCell(c)){const _has=csHas(val);body+=`<td class="cell cscell" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}"${span}${st?` style="${st}"`:''}><button class="cs-btn${_has?' has':''}" data-csr="${ri}">${_has?'DETAIL':'ADD'}</button></td>`;}
      else if(!isHdr&&isDaysCell(c)){const _d=doDaysAfter(ri);body+=`<td class="cell aqcell" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}"${span}${st?` style="${st}"`:''}>${_d===''?'':esc(String(_d))}</td>`;}
      else if(!isHdr&&isDupSheet()&&dupDDOpts(c)){const _cv=val==null?'':String(val);body+=`<td class="cell ddcell" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}"${span}${st?` style="${st}"`:''}><span class="ddval">${esc(_cv)}</span><span class="ddcaret" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}">\u25be</span></td>`;}
      else if(!isHdr&&isDupSheet()&&dupCfg().truck!=null&&c===dupCfg().truck){const _has=tkParse(val).length>0;body+=`<td class="cell tkcell" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}"${span}${st?` style="${st}"`:''}><button class="ad-btn${_has?' has':''}" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}">${_has?'Detail':'Add'}</button></td>`;}
      else if(!isHdr&&isDupSheet()&&dupCfg().deliv!=null&&c===dupCfg().deliv){const _has=String(val||'').trim()!=='';body+=`<td class="cell adcell" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}"${span}${st?` style="${st}"`:''}><button class="ad-btn${_has?' has':''}" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}">${_has?'Detail':'Add'}</button></td>`;}
      else if(isDoneCell){
        const chk=(val==='TRUE')?'checked':'';
        body+=`<td class="cell bool${isDoneCell?' opsdone':''}" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}"${span}${st?` style="${st}"`:''}><input type="checkbox" class="bchk${isDoneCell?' opsdone-chk':''}" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}" ${chk}></td>`;
      }else{
        const al=cellAlign(f);if(al!=='center')st+=`text-align:${al};`;
        body+=`<td class="cell${(!isHdr&&efaLockedCol(c))?' lockedsrc':''}" data-r="${ri}" data-c="${c}"${span}${st?` style="${st}"`:''}>${esc(fmtDisplay(val,f))}</td>`;
      }
    }
    body+='</tr>';
  }
  $('#rowbar').innerHTML=`<table><tbody>${rb}</tbody></table>`;
  const remain=idx.length-shown.length;
  let lm=remain>0?`<div class="loadmore"><button id="loadMore">Load ${Math.min(PAGE,remain)} more rows</button></div>`:'';
  $('#ssheet').innerHTML=`<table style="width:${totalW}px"><colgroup>${cols}</colgroup><tbody>${body}</tbody></table>${lm}`;
  $('#ssheet').scrollLeft=_keepL;$('#ssheet').scrollTop=_keepT;$('#colbar').scrollLeft=_keepL;$('#rowbar').scrollTop=_keepT;
  syncRowHeights();
  applyFreeze();
  const lmb=document.getElementById('loadMore');if(lmb)lmb.onclick=()=>{
    const i2=filteredRows();
    if(i2.length>visible){visible+=PAGE}
    else{const n2=normalize(active);for(let i=0;i<PAGE;i++)DATA[active].push(new Array(n2).fill(''));if(ROWH[active])ROWH[active].length=DATA[active].length;visible+=PAGE;save()}
    renderSheet();
  };
  highlight();
  markNotes();
}
function applyFreeze(){
  const sw=$('#ssheet'),cb=$('#colbar'),rbEl=$('#rowbar');
  if(!sw)return;
  const sheetTbl=sw.querySelector('table');
  const colTbl=cb?cb.querySelector('table'):null;
  const rowTbl=rbEl?rbEl.querySelector('table'):null;
  if(!sheetTbl)return;
  const fz=freezeOf(active);
  const fzc=(fz.c||0);
  if(fz.r<=0&&fzc<=0)return;
  const ths=colTbl?Array.from(colTbl.querySelectorAll('th[data-col]')):[];
  const leftOf={};const fcSet=new Set();let lastFcPc=-1;let cum=0;
  ths.forEach((t,i)=>{const pc=+t.dataset.col;if(i<fzc){leftOf[pc]=cum;fcSet.add(pc);lastFcPc=pc;}cum+=t.offsetWidth;});
  ths.forEach(t=>{const pc=+t.dataset.col;if(fcSet.has(pc)){t.style.position='sticky';t.style.left=leftOf[pc]+'px';t.style.zIndex='7';t.style.background='var(--surface)';if(pc===lastFcPc)t.style.boxShadow='2px 0 0 0 var(--green-d,#008F4C)';}});
  const bodyRows=Array.from(sheetTbl.querySelectorAll('tbody>tr'));
  const frCount=Math.min(fz.r,bodyRows.length);
  const topOf=[];let cumT=0;
  bodyRows.forEach((tr,ri)=>{if(ri<frCount)topOf[ri]=cumT;cumT+=tr.offsetHeight;});
  const lastFrIdx=frCount-1;
  bodyRows.forEach((tr,ri)=>{
    const isFr=ri<frCount;const isLastFr=ri===lastFrIdx;
    Array.from(tr.children).forEach(td=>{
      const pc=+td.getAttribute('data-c');
      const isFc=fcSet.has(pc);const isLastFc=pc===lastFcPc;
      if(isFc){td.style.position='sticky';td.style.left=leftOf[pc]+'px';if(!td.style.background)td.style.background='var(--surface)';}
      if(isFr){td.style.position='sticky';td.style.top=topOf[ri]+'px';if(!td.style.background)td.style.background='var(--surface)';}
      if(isFr&&isFc)td.style.zIndex='8';else if(isFc)td.style.zIndex='5';else if(isFr)td.style.zIndex='6';
      let bsh='';if(isLastFc&&fzc>0)bsh+='2px 0 0 0 var(--green-d,#008F4C)';if(isLastFr&&fz.r>0)bsh+=(bsh?',':'')+'0 2px 0 0 var(--green-d,#008F4C)';if(bsh)td.style.boxShadow=bsh;
    });
  });
  if(rowTbl){
    const rrows=Array.from(rowTbl.querySelectorAll('tbody>tr'));
    rrows.forEach((tr,ri)=>{if(ri<frCount){Array.from(tr.children).forEach(td=>{td.style.position='sticky';td.style.top=topOf[ri]+'px';td.style.zIndex='7';td.style.background='#e7ece5';if(ri===lastFrIdx)td.style.boxShadow='0 2px 0 0 var(--green-d,#008F4C)';});}});
  }
}
function syncRowHeights(){
  const bt=$('#ssheet').querySelector('table'),rt=$('#rowbar').querySelector('table');if(!bt||!rt||!bt.tBodies[0]||!rt.tBodies[0])return;
  const br=bt.tBodies[0].rows,rr=rt.tBodies[0].rows;
  for(let i=0;i<rr.length&&i<br.length;i++){const h=br[i].offsetHeight;rr[i].style.height=h+'px';if(rr[i].cells[0])rr[i].cells[0].style.height=h+'px'}
}

/* ---------- SELECTION (Excel-like: drag cells/rows/cols, select-all) ---------- */
function selRect(){
  if(!sel)return null;
  const R=DATA[active].length, n=efaNCols(active);
  let r1,r2,c1,c2;
  if(sel.kind==='all'){r1=0;r2=R-1;c1=0;c2=n-1}
  else if(sel.kind==='col'){c1=Math.min(sel.a.c,sel.f.c);c2=Math.max(sel.a.c,sel.f.c);r1=0;r2=R-1}
  else if(sel.kind==='row'){r1=Math.min(sel.a.r,sel.f.r);r2=Math.max(sel.a.r,sel.f.r);c1=0;c2=n-1}
  else{r1=Math.min(sel.a.r,sel.f.r);r2=Math.max(sel.a.r,sel.f.r);c1=Math.min(sel.a.c,sel.f.c);c2=Math.max(sel.a.c,sel.f.c)}
  return{r1,r2,c1,c2};
}
function highlight(){
  const ss=$('#ssheet'),cb=$('#colbar'),rbw=$('#rowbar');if(!ss.querySelector('table')){$('#selInfo').textContent='';return}
  ss.querySelectorAll('td.cell.sel-rng').forEach(td=>td.classList.remove('sel-rng'));
  rbw.querySelectorAll('td.rownum.sel').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('sel'));
  cb.querySelectorAll('th.sel').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('sel'));
  const rect=selRect();
  if(!rect){$('#selInfo').textContent='';updateFmtToolbar();return}
  ss.querySelectorAll('td.cell').forEach(td=>{const r=+td.dataset.r,c=+td.dataset.c;if(r>=rect.r1&&r<=rect.r2&&c>=rect.c1&&c<=rect.c2)td.classList.add('sel-rng')});
  cb.querySelectorAll('th[data-col]').forEach(th=>{const c=+th.dataset.col;if(c>=rect.c1&&c<=rect.c2)th.classList.add('sel')});
  rbw.querySelectorAll('td.rownum[data-row]').forEach(rn=>{const r=+rn.dataset.row;if(r>=rect.r1&&r<=rect.r2)rn.classList.add('sel')});
  const rr=rect.r2-rect.r1+1,cc=rect.c2-rect.c1+1;
  $('#selInfo').textContent=(rr*cc>1)?`${rr}×${cc}`:'';
  updateFmtToolbar();
}
function setActiveBtn(id,on){const el=$('#'+id);if(el)el.classList.toggle('active',on)}
function updateFmtToolbar(){
  let f={};const rect=selRect();
  if(rect)f=fmtOf(rect.r1,rect.c1)||{};
  else if(lastRC)f=fmtOf(lastRC.r,lastRC.c)||{};
  const type=f.type||'text';
  const align=f.align||(type==='number'?'right':'center');
  setActiveBtn('fmtText',type==='text');setActiveBtn('fmtNum',type==='number');setActiveBtn('fmtTime',type==='time');setActiveBtn('fmtDate',type==='date');
  setActiveBtn('alLeft',align==='left');setActiveBtn('alCenter',align==='center');setActiveBtn('alRight',align==='right');
}
function cellEl(r,c){return $('#ssheet').querySelector(`td.cell[data-r="${r}"][data-c="${c}"]`)}
/* clipboard */
function copyText(t){const done=()=>toast('Copied to clipboard');if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).then(done).catch(()=>fbCopy(t,done))}else fbCopy(t,done)}
function fbCopy(t,done){const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=t;ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.focus();ta.select();let ok=false;try{ok=document.execCommand('copy')}catch(e){}document.body.removeChild(ta);ok?done():toast('Copy not allowed here')}
function copySel(){
  const rect=selRect();if(!rect){toast('Nothing selected');return}
  const out=[];for(let r=rect.r1;r<=rect.r2;r++){const a=[];for(let c=rect.c1;c<=rect.c2;c++){const v=DATA[active][r][c];a.push(v==null?'':v)}out.push(a.join('\t'))}
  copyText(out.join('\n'));
}
function deleteSel(){
  const rect=selRect();if(!rect||!sel){toast('Nothing selected');return}
  if(sel.kind==='col'){const ord=buildColOrd(active,efaNCols(active));const ia=ord.indexOf(sel.a.c),ib=ord.indexOf(sel.f.c);if(ia<0||ib<0){toast('Select a column first');return;}const lo=Math.min(ia,ib),hi=Math.max(ia,ib);const toHide=ord.slice(lo,hi+1);if(toHide.length>=ord.length){toast('Cannot delete all columns');return;}const what=`${toHide.length} column(s)`;confirmBox(`Delete ${what}?`,()=>{const set=new Set(XHIDE[active]||[]);toHide.forEach(c=>set.add(c));XHIDE[active]=[...set];sel=null;saveXHide();save();renderSheet();toast(`Deleted ${what}`)})}
  else if(sel.kind==='row'){const what=`${rect.r2-rect.r1+1} row(s)`;confirmBox(`Delete ${what}?`,()=>{for(let r=rect.r2;r>=rect.r1;r--){if(r===0)continue;DATA[active].splice(r,1);if(ROWH[active])ROWH[active].splice(r,1)}sel=null;save();saveH();renderSidebar();renderSheet();toast(`Deleted ${what}`)})}
  else{confirmBox('Clear contents of the selected cells?',()=>{for(let r=rect.r1;r<=rect.r2;r++)for(let c=rect.c1;c<=rect.c2;c++){if(efaLockedCol(c))continue;DATA[active][r][c]='';}save();renderSheet();toast('Selection cleared')})}
}
function fillDown(){
  const rect=selRect();if(!rect)return;
  let r1=rect.r1,r2=rect.r2,c1=rect.c1,c2=rect.c2;if(r1===0)r1=1;if(r1>r2)return;
  const single=(rect.r1===rect.r2);
  const src=single?(r1-1):r1;if(src<1)return;
  const start=single?r1:(r1+1);if(start>r2)return;
  for(let r=start;r<=r2;r++){if(r===0)continue;for(let c=c1;c<=c2;c++){
    if(efaLockedCol(c))continue;
    DATA[active][r][c]=DATA[active][src][c];
    const sf=fmtOf(src,c);const key=r+':'+c;if(!FMT[active])FMT[active]={};if(sf)FMT[active][key]=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(sf));else delete FMT[active][key];
  }}
  save();saveF();renderSheet();
}
function copyDupRows(){
  const rect=selRect();if(!rect||!sel){toast('Select a row first');return;}
  let r1=Math.max(1,rect.r1),r2=rect.r2;if(r2<1){toast('Select a row first');return;}
  const rows=DATA[active];const clones=[];for(let r=r1;r<=r2;r++){if(rows[r])clones.push(rows[r].slice());}
  if(!clones.length)return;const pos=r2+1,cnt=clones.length;
  if(FMT[active]){const nf={};Object.keys(FMT[active]).forEach(kk=>{const mm=kk.split(':');const rr=+mm[0],cc=+mm[1];const nr=rr>=pos?rr+cnt:rr;nf[nr+':'+cc]=FMT[active][kk];});FMT[active]=nf;saveF();}
  if(ROWH[active]){const hs=[];for(let k=0;k<cnt;k++)hs.push(ROWH[active][r1+k]);ROWH[active].splice(pos,0,...hs);if(typeof saveH==='function')saveH();}
  if(ESEQ[active]){ensureESeq(active);const seq=ESEQ[active];let mx=0;for(let i=0;i<seq.length;i++)if(seq[i]>mx)mx=seq[i];const ns=[];for(let k=0;k<cnt;k++)ns.push(++mx);seq.splice(pos,0,...ns);saveESeq();}
  if(MERGES[active]&&MERGES[active].length){MERGES[active]=[];saveM();}
  rows.splice(pos,0,...clones);visible=Math.max(visible,rows.length);sel=null;lastRC=null;save();renderSidebar();renderSheet();toast(cnt>1?(cnt+' rows duplicated'):'Row duplicated');
}

$('#bulkDelete').onclick=deleteSel;
/* ---------- clipboard: copy / cut / paste across grid cells ---------- */
function selToTSV(){const rect=selRect();if(!rect)return null;const out=[];for(let r=rect.r1;r<=rect.r2;r++){const a=[];for(let c=rect.c1;c<=rect.c2;c++){const v=DATA[active][r][c];a.push(v==null?'':String(v))}out.push(a.join('\t'))}return out.join('\n');}
function applyPaste(text){
  if(text==null||text==='')return;
  const rect=selRect();let R0,C0;
  if(rect){R0=rect.r1;C0=rect.c1}else if(lastRC){R0=lastRC.r;C0=lastRC.c}else{toast('Chọn ô để dán');return}
  const lines=text.replace(/\r\n/g,'\n').replace(/\r/g,'\n').split('\n');
  if(lines.length&&lines[lines.length-1]==='')lines.pop();
  if(!lines.length)return;
  const grid=lines.map(l=>l.split('\t'));const n=efaNCols(active);
  const need=R0+grid.length;while(DATA[active].length<need)DATA[active].push(new Array(n).fill(''));
  if(ROWH[active])ROWH[active].length=DATA[active].length;
  let maxC=C0;
  for(let i=0;i<grid.length;i++){const ln=grid[i];for(let j=0;j<ln.length;j++){const c=C0+j;if(c>=n)break;if(efaLockedCol(c))continue;DATA[active][R0+i][c]=ln[j];if(c>maxC)maxC=c;}}
  const r2=Math.min(R0+grid.length-1,DATA[active].length-1);
  const idx=filteredRows(),pos=idx.indexOf(r2);if(pos>=visible)visible=pos+1;
  sel={kind:'cell',a:{r:R0,c:C0},f:{r:r2,c:Math.min(maxC,n-1)}};lastRC={r:R0,c:C0};
  save();renderSheet();toast('Pasted');
}
function _gridClipActive(){if(page!=='sheet'||!active||active==='VBPL'||(typeof CHECKLIST_SET!=='undefined'&&CHECKLIST_SET.has(active)))return false;const ae=document.activeElement;if(ae&&(ae.isContentEditable||/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(ae.tagName)))return false;return true;}
document.addEventListener('copy',e=>{if(!_gridClipActive()||!sel)return;const tsv=selToTSV();if(tsv==null)return;e.preventDefault();(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData).setData('text/plain',tsv);toast('Copied');});
document.addEventListener('cut',e=>{if(!_gridClipActive()||!sel)return;const tsv=selToTSV();if(tsv==null)return;e.preventDefault();(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData).setData('text/plain',tsv);const rect=selRect();for(let r=rect.r1;r<=rect.r2;r++)for(let c=rect.c1;c<=rect.c2;c++){if(efaLockedCol(c))continue;DATA[active][r][c]='';}save();renderSheet();toast('Cut');});
document.addEventListener('paste',e=>{if(!_gridClipActive())return;if(!sel&&!lastRC)return;const t=(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData).getData('text');if(t==null||t==='')return;e.preventDefault();applyPaste(t);});
const _pasteBtn=document.getElementById('bulkPaste');if(_pasteBtn)_pasteBtn.onclick=()=>{if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.readText){navigator.clipboard.readText().then(t=>{if(t)applyPaste(t);else toast('Clipboard rỗng');}).catch(()=>toast('Nhấn Ctrl+V để dán'));}else toast('Nhấn Ctrl+V để dán');};

/* drag selection (rows / columns / all) */
function startSel(kind,r,c){sel={kind,a:{r,c},f:{r,c}};lastRC={r,c};dragType=kind;dragging=true;highlight()}
/* extend selection to a screen point (shared by drag + edge auto-scroll) */
function extendSelAtPoint(x,y){
  const el=document.elementFromPoint(x,y);if(!el||!el.closest)return;
  if(dragType==='col'){const th=el.closest('th[data-col]');if(th){const c=+th.dataset.col;if(c!==sel.f.c){sel.f.c=c;highlight()}}}
  else if(dragType==='row'){const rn=el.closest('td.rownum[data-row]');if(rn){const r=+rn.dataset.row;if(r!==sel.f.r){sel.f.r=r;highlight()}}}
  else if(dragType==='cellctrl'){const cl=el.closest('td.cell');if(cl){const r=+cl.dataset.r,c=+cl.dataset.c,o=sel.a;sel.kind=Math.abs(r-o.r)>=Math.abs(c-o.c)?'row':'col';sel.f={r,c};highlight()}}
  else if(dragType==='cell'){const cl=el.closest('td.cell');if(cl){const r=+cl.dataset.r,c=+cl.dataset.c;if(r!==sel.f.r||c!==sel.f.c){cellMoved=true;sel.f={r,c};highlight()}}}
}
/* auto-scroll the grid when the pointer is held near/over an edge during a drag-select */
let dragClientX=0,dragClientY=0,autoRAF=null;
function startAutoScroll(){if(autoRAF==null)autoRAF=requestAnimationFrame(autoScrollStep);}
function autoScrollStep(){
  if(!dragging||!sel||dragType==='all'){autoRAF=null;return;}
  const ss=$('#ssheet');if(!ss){autoRAF=null;return;}
  const rect=ss.getBoundingClientRect();const EDGE=44,MAX=28;let vx=0,vy=0;
  if(dragType!=='row'){
    if(dragClientX>rect.right-EDGE)vx=Math.ceil((dragClientX-(rect.right-EDGE))/EDGE*MAX);
    else if(dragClientX<rect.left+EDGE)vx=-Math.ceil(((rect.left+EDGE)-dragClientX)/EDGE*MAX);
  }
  if(dragType!=='col'){
    if(dragClientY>rect.bottom-EDGE)vy=Math.ceil((dragClientY-(rect.bottom-EDGE))/EDGE*MAX);
    else if(dragClientY<rect.top+EDGE)vy=-Math.ceil(((rect.top+EDGE)-dragClientY)/EDGE*MAX);
  }
  if(vx){const mx=ss.scrollWidth-ss.clientWidth;ss.scrollLeft=Math.max(0,Math.min(mx,ss.scrollLeft+vx));}
  if(vy){const my=ss.scrollHeight-ss.clientHeight;ss.scrollTop=Math.max(0,Math.min(my,ss.scrollTop+vy));}
  if(vx||vy){
    const sx=vx?Math.max(rect.left+5,Math.min(rect.right-5,dragClientX)):dragClientX;
    const sy=vy?Math.max(rect.top+5,Math.min(rect.bottom-5,dragClientY)):dragClientY;
    extendSelAtPoint(sx,sy);
  }
  autoRAF=requestAnimationFrame(autoScrollStep);
}
document.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
  if(!dragging||!sel||dragType==='all')return;
  dragClientX=e.clientX;dragClientY=e.clientY;
  extendSelAtPoint(e.clientX,e.clientY);
  startAutoScroll();
});
document.addEventListener('mouseup',()=>{
  if(dragType==='cell'&&!cellMoved&&downCell){lastRC={r:+downCell.dataset.r,c:+downCell.dataset.c}}
  dragging=false;dragType=null;downCell=null;cellMoved=false;
  if(autoRAF){cancelAnimationFrame(autoRAF);autoRAF=null;}
});
let editFull=false;let ddEditPrev=null;let ddFreeEdit=false;
function beginEdit(cell,full){if(!cell||cell.classList.contains('bool'))return;if(cell.classList.contains('ddcell')||cell.classList.contains('adcell')||cell.classList.contains('aqcell')||cell.classList.contains('tkcell')||cell.classList.contains('sendcell')||cell.classList.contains('cscell')||cell.classList.contains('ddcell'))return;editFull=!!full;
  const r=+cell.dataset.r,c=+cell.dataset.c;lastRC={r,c};
  if(c===tsPhys(active)){if(typeof toast==='function')toast('🔒 Auto time — read only');return;}
  if(efaLockedCol(c)){if(typeof toast==='function')toast('🔒 Column locked');return;}
  if(isOpsSheet(active)&&opsBaseOf(active)==='EFA'&&typeof efaNoFutureCols==='function'){const _nf=efaNoFutureCols();if(_nf&&_nf.has(c)){openDate(cell);return;}}
  if(isEFASheet()&&EFA_DD[c]&&efaFull())ddEditPrev=String(DATA[active][r][c]==null?'':DATA[active][r][c]);
  cell.textContent=DATA[active][r][c]==null?'':String(DATA[active][r][c]);
  cell.setAttribute('contenteditable','true');cell.classList.add('editing');cell.focus();
  const rng=document.createRange();rng.selectNodeContents(cell);rng.collapse(false);const s=getSelection();s.removeAllRanges();s.addRange(rng);}
function cellEl(r,c){return $('#ssheet').querySelector(`td.cell[data-r="${r}"][data-c="${c}"]`)}
function moveSel(dr,dc){
  if(!sel||sel.kind!=='cell')return;const R=DATA[active].length,n=efaNCols(active);
  const nr=Math.max(0,Math.min(R-1,sel.f.r+dr));let nc=Math.max(0,Math.min(n-1,sel.f.c+dc));
  if(dc!==0&&typeof efaHiddenCol==='function'){const step=dc>0?1:-1;while(nc>=0&&nc<=n-1&&efaHiddenCol(nc))nc+=step;if(nc<0||nc>n-1||efaHiddenCol(nc))nc=sel.f.c;}
  sel={kind:'cell',a:{r:nr,c:nc},f:{r:nr,c:nc}};
  const idx=filteredRows(),pos=idx.indexOf(nr);if(pos>=visible){visible=pos+1;renderSheet()}else highlight();
  const c=cellEl(nr,nc);if(c)c.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',inline:'nearest'});
}
$('#ssheet').addEventListener('blur',e=>{const c=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('td.cell');if(c&&c.isContentEditable){const r=+c.dataset.r,col=+c.dataset.c;c.removeAttribute('contenteditable');c.classList.remove('editing');c.textContent=fmtDisplay(DATA[active][r][col],fmtOf(r,col));if(isEFASheet()){renderSheet();}else{syncRowHeights();}}},true);

let confirmCb=null;
function confirmBox(msg,cb,okTxt,cancelTxt){$('#confirmMsg').textContent=msg;$('#confirmOk').textContent=okTxt||'Confirm';$('#confirmCancel').textContent=cancelTxt||'Cancel';confirmCb=cb;$('#confirmOverlay').classList.add('on')}
function closeConfirm(){$('#confirmOverlay').classList.remove('on');confirmCb=null}

document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if($('#confirmOverlay').classList.contains('on')){if(e.key==='Escape')closeConfirm();return}
  const ed=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('td.cell[contenteditable="true"]');
  if(ed){
    if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();ed.blur();return}
    const r=+ed.dataset.r,c=+ed.dataset.c;
    if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();ed.blur();sel={kind:'cell',a:{r,c},f:{r,c}};moveSel(1,0);return}
    if(e.key==='Tab'){e.preventDefault();ed.blur();sel={kind:'cell',a:{r,c},f:{r,c}};moveSel(0,e.shiftKey?-1:1);return}
    const d={ArrowUp:[-1,0],ArrowDown:[1,0],ArrowLeft:[0,-1],ArrowRight:[0,1]}[e.key];
    if(d){e.preventDefault();ed.blur();sel={kind:'cell',a:{r,c},f:{r,c}};moveSel(d[0],d[1]);return}
    return;
  }
  if(e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('input,textarea'))return;
  /* Ctrl+C handled by the native copy event listener below */
  if(!sel){return}
  if(e.key==='Escape'){clearSel();return}
  if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&(e.key==='d'||e.key==='D')){e.preventDefault();fillDown();return}
  if(sel.kind==='cell'&&sel.a.r===sel.f.r&&sel.a.c===sel.f.c){
    const cell=cellEl(sel.f.r,sel.f.c);
    if(isEFASheet()&&efaLockedCol(sel.f.c)&&(e.key==='Enter'||e.key==='F2'||e.key==='Delete'||e.key==='Backspace'||(e.key.length===1&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey&&!e.altKey))){e.preventDefault();if(typeof toast==='function')toast('🔒 Source column — edit in the ECD tab');return;}
    if(isEFASheet()){const _c=sel.f.c;
      if(EFA_DD[_c]&&efaFull()){if(e.key==='Delete'||e.key==='Backspace'){e.preventDefault();DATA[active][sel.f.r][_c]='';save();renderSheet();return}if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key==='F2'){e.preventDefault();if(cell)openDD(cell,'');return}if(e.key.length===1&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey&&!e.altKey){e.preventDefault();if(cell)openDD(cell,e.key);return}}
      else if(_c===EFA_AD_COL&&efaFull()){if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key==='F2'){e.preventDefault();openADModal(sel.f.r,true);return}if(e.key.length===1||e.key==='Delete'||e.key==='Backspace'){e.preventDefault();return}}
      else if(_c===EFA_AQ_COL&&efaFull()){if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key==='F2'||e.key.length===1||e.key==='Delete'||e.key==='Backspace'){e.preventDefault();return}}
    }
    if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();beginEdit(cell,false);return}
    if(e.key==='F2'){e.preventDefault();beginEdit(cell,true);return}
    if(e.key==='Delete'||e.key==='Backspace'){e.preventDefault();DATA[active][sel.f.r][sel.f.c]='';if(cell)cell.textContent='';save();return}
    const d={ArrowUp:[-1,0],ArrowDown:[1,0],ArrowLeft:[0,-1],ArrowRight:[0,1]}[e.key];
    if(d){e.preventDefault();moveSel(d[0],d[1]);return}
    if(e.key.length===1&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey&&!e.altKey){e.preventDefault();if(cell){beginEdit(cell);try{document.execCommand('selectAll',false,null);document.execCommand('insertText',false,e.key)}catch(_){cell.textContent=e.key}DATA[active][sel.f.r][sel.f.c]=cell.innerText;save()}}
  }
});

/* checkbox TRUE/FALSE */
$('#ssheet').addEventListener('change',e=>{
  const _ds=e.target.closest('.dd-sel');if(_ds){DATA[active][+_ds.dataset.r][+_ds.dataset.c]=_ds.value;save();return;}
  const cb=e.target.closest('.bchk');if(!cb)return;
  if(cb.classList.contains('opsdone-chk')){opsToggleDone(active,+cb.dataset.r,cb.checked);return;}
  const _r=+cb.dataset.r,_c=+cb.dataset.c;
  DATA[active][_r][_c]=cb.checked?'TRUE':'FALSE';
  save();
  if(_c===27&&opsBaseOf(active)==='EFA'){if(cb.checked){efaPushToCCD(active,_r);}else if(!efaUntickBCSent(active,_r)){DATA[active][_r][_c]='TRUE';save();cb.checked=true;if(typeof toast==='function')toast("In progress, can't untick");}}
  if(_c===27&&opsBaseOf(active)==='FCF'){const _pp=opsParts(active);if(_pp&&_pp.dept==='ECD'){if(cb.checked){fcfPush(active,_r,'DCD');}else if(!fcfUntick(active,_r,'DCD')){DATA[active][_r][_c]='TRUE';save();cb.checked=true;if(typeof toast==='function')toast("In progress, can't untick");}}else if(_pp&&_pp.dept==='DCD'){if(cb.checked){fcfPush(active,_r,'FCD');}else if(!fcfUntick(active,_r,'FCD')){DATA[active][_r][_c]='TRUE';save();cb.checked=true;if(typeof toast==='function')toast("In progress, can't untick");}}}
  if(_c===28&&opsBaseOf(active)==='DO'){const _dp=opsParts(active);if(_dp&&_dp.dept==='ICD'){if(cb.checked){doPush(active,_r);}else if(!doUntick(active,_r)){DATA[active][_r][_c]='TRUE';save();cb.checked=true;if(typeof toast==='function')toast("In progress, can't untick");}}}
  if(_c===26&&isCCDSheet()){if(cb.checked){efaPushToDCD(active,_r);}else if(!efaUntickStuffing(active,_r)){DATA[active][_r][_c]='TRUE';save();cb.checked=true;if(typeof toast==='function')toast("In progress, can't untick");}}
  if(_c===26&&isDCDSheet()){if(cb.checked){efaPushToFCD(active,_r);}else if(!efaUntickFCD(active,_r)){DATA[active][_r][_c]='TRUE';save();cb.checked=true;if(typeof toast==='function')toast("In progress, can't untick");}}
  if(_c===xferCol(active)){if(cb.checked)xferTransfer(active,_r);else xferUntransfer(active,_r);}
});

    const ed=$('#ssheet').querySelector('td.cell[contenteditable="true"]');if(ed)ed.blur();

    if(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey){sel={kind:'row',a:{r,c},f:{r,c}};dragType='cellctrl';dragging=true;highlight();e.preventDefault();return}
    if(e.shiftKey){const an=(sel&&sel.kind==='cell')?sel.a:(lastRC||{r,c});sel={kind:'cell',a:{r:an.r,c:an.c},f:{r,c}};dragType='cell';dragging=true;cellMoved=true;downCell=cl;highlight();e.preventDefault();return}
    sel={kind:'cell',a:{r,c},f:{r,c}};dragType='cell';dragging=true;cellMoved=false;downCell=cl;highlight();e.preventDefault();
  }
});

/* ---------- date picker ---------- */
let dpCell=null,dpView=new Date();
function openDate(cell){
  const ed=$('#ssheet').querySelector('td.cell[contenteditable="true"]');if(ed)ed.blur();
  dpCell=cell;const r=+cell.dataset.r,c=+cell.dataset.c;lastRC={r,c};
  dpView=parseDate(String(DATA[active][r][c]||''))||new Date();
  renderDate();const dp=$('#datepop');dp.style.display='block';
  const rc=cell.getBoundingClientRect();
  dp.style.left=Math.max(8,Math.min(rc.left,window.innerWidth-250))+'px';
  dp.style.top=Math.min(rc.bottom+4,window.innerHeight-300)+'px';
}
function closeDate(){$('#datepop').style.display='none';dpCell=null}
function renderDate(){
  const y=dpView.getFullYear(),m=dpView.getMonth();
  const MM=['January','February','March','April','May','June','July','August','September','October','November','December'];
  const first=new Date(y,m,1).getDay(),days=new Date(y,m+1,0).getDate();
  const today=new Date();const sel=dpCell?parseDate(String(DATA[active][+dpCell.dataset.r][+dpCell.dataset.c]||'')):null;
  let h=`<div class="dp-head"><button class="dp-nav" data-dp="prev">‹</button><span class="dp-title">${MM[m]} ${y}</span><button class="dp-nav" data-dp="next">›</button></div><div class="dp-grid">`;
  ['Su','Mo','Tu','We','Th','Fr','Sa'].forEach(d=>h+=`<span class="dp-w">${d}</span>`);
  for(let i=0;i<first;i++)h+='<span></span>';
  let _dpMax=null;if(dpCell&&typeof doNoFuture==='function'&&doNoFuture(+dpCell.dataset.c)){const _tt=new Date();_tt.setHours(23,59,59,999);_dpMax=_tt.getTime();}
  for(let d=1;d<=days;d++){
    const isT=today.getFullYear()===y&&today.getMonth()===m&&today.getDate()===d;
    const isS=sel&&sel.getFullYear()===y&&sel.getMonth()===m&&sel.getDate()===d;
    const _dis=_dpMax!=null&&new Date(y,m,d,12,0,0,0).getTime()>_dpMax;
    h+=`<button class="dp-d ${isS?'sel':''} ${isT?'today':''} ${_dis?'dis':''}" ${_dis?'disabled':`data-day="${d}"`}>${d}</button>`;
  }
  h+='</div><div class="dp-foot"><button class="dp-clear" data-dp="clear">Clear</button><button class="dp-today" data-dp="now">Today</button></div>';
  $('#datepop').innerHTML=h;
}
function applyDate(dt){
  if(!dpCell)return;const r=+dpCell.dataset.r,c=+dpCell.dataset.c;
  if(dt&&typeof doNoFuture==='function'&&doNoFuture(c)){const _tt=new Date();_tt.setHours(23,59,59,999);if(dt.getTime()>_tt.getTime())return;}
  const iso=dt?`${dt.getFullYear()}-${String(dt.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(dt.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')}`:'';
  DATA[active][r][c]=iso;
  if(!FMT[active])FMT[active]={};FMT[active][r+':'+c]=Object.assign(FMT[active][r+':'+c]||{},{type:'date'});
  save();saveF();renderSheet();closeDate();
}
$('#datepop').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const nav=e.target.closest('[data-dp]');
  if(nav){const a=nav.dataset.dp;
    if(a==='prev'){dpView=new Date(dpView.getFullYear(),dpView.getMonth()-1,1);renderDate();return}
    if(a==='next'){dpView=new Date(dpView.getFullYear(),dpView.getMonth()+1,1);renderDate();return}
    if(a==='clear'){applyDate(null);return}
    if(a==='now'){applyDate(new Date());return}
  }
  const d=e.target.closest('[data-day]');
  if(d)applyDate(new Date(dpView.getFullYear(),dpView.getMonth(),+d.dataset.day));
});
document.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{if($('#datepop').style.display==='block'&&!e.target.closest('#datepop'))closeDate()});

$('#ssheet').addEventListener('scroll',()=>{$('#colbar').scrollLeft=$('#ssheet').scrollLeft;$('#rowbar').scrollTop=$('#ssheet').scrollTop;if(active)SCROLLPOS[active]={l:$('#ssheet').scrollLeft,t:$('#ssheet').scrollTop};});

$('#btnAddCol').onclick=()=>{
  const n0=normalize(active);
  let after=null;
  if(sel&&sel.kind==='col'&&sel.a&&sel.f)after=Math.max(sel.a.c,sel.f.c);
  else if(sel&&sel.f&&typeof sel.f.c==='number'&&sel.f.c>=0)after=sel.f.c;
  else if(lastRC&&typeof lastRC.c==='number'&&lastRC.c>=0)after=lastRC.c;
  const _vc=(typeof efaVisCols==='function')?efaVisCols(active):0;
  const phys=(_vc>0)?Math.max(n0,_vc+1):n0;
  DATA[active].forEach(r=>{while(r.length<phys)r.push('');r.push('');});
  if(COLW[active]){while(COLW[active].length<phys)COLW[active].push(DEFAULT_CW);COLW[active].push(DEFAULT_CW);}
  XADD[active]=XADD[active]||[];XADD[active].push({c:phys,after:after});
  saveXAdd();save();renderSheet();
  sel={kind:'col',a:{r:0,c:phys},f:{r:0,c:phys}};lastRC={r:0,c:phys};highlight();
  toast(after==null?'Column added at the end':'Column added');
};

};
$('#freezeCol').onclick=()=>{

  else if(sel&&sel.f&&typeof sel.f.c==='number')cc=sel.f.c;

/* export csv */
function exportCSV(filtered){
  const ord=buildColOrd(active,efaNCols(active));
  const idx=filtered?filteredRows():DATA[active].map((r,i)=>i);
  const rows=idx.map(ri=>{const r=DATA[active][ri];return ord.map(c=>`"${String(r[c]==null?'':r[c]).replace(/"/g,'""')}"`).join(',');});
  const csv='\uFEFF'+rows.join('\r\n');
  dl(new Blob([csv],{type:'text/csv'}),`SHOPTRANS_${active}${filtered?'_filtered':''}.csv`);toast(filtered?'Filtered CSV exported':'CSV exported');
}
function closeExportMenu(){const m=document.getElementById('exportMenu');if(m)m.remove();}
$('#btnExport').onclick=(e)=>{
  e.stopPropagation();
  if(document.getElementById('exportMenu')){closeExportMenu();return;}
  const m=document.createElement('div');m.id='exportMenu';m.className='export-menu';
  m.innerHTML=`<button data-exp="all">Export All</button><button data-exp="filter">Export Filtered</button>`;
  document.body.appendChild(m);
  const b=$('#btnExport').getBoundingClientRect();
  m.style.top=(b.bottom+4)+'px';m.style.left=Math.max(8,b.right-m.offsetWidth)+'px';
  m.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=(ev)=>{ev.stopPropagation();exportCSV(btn.dataset.exp==='filter');closeExportMenu();});
};
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(!e.target.closest('#exportMenu')&&!e.target.closest('#btnExport'))closeExportMenu();});

function migrateFcf(){
  let changed=false;
  for(const leaf of SHEETS.slice()){
    if(leaf.indexOf('#')>=0)continue;
    const p=opsParts(leaf);if(!p||(p.base!=='FCF'&&p.base!=='DO'&&p.base!=='DUP'))continue;
    const want=opsLeafHeader(p.base,p.dept,p.mode);
    const cur=DATA[leaf]&&DATA[leaf][0];
    const ok=cur&&cur.length>=want.length&&want.every((h,i)=>cur[i]===h);
    if(ok)continue;
    [leaf,opsDoneKey(leaf),opsPendKey(leaf)].forEach(k=>{const _old=DATA[k]||[];const _rows=_old.slice(1).map(r=>{const nr=(r||[]).slice(0,want.length);while(nr.length<want.length)nr.push('');return nr;});DATA[k]=[want.slice(),..._rows];try{if(FMT&&typeof FMT==='object')delete FMT[k];}catch(e){}try{if(ESEQ&&typeof ESEQ==='object')delete ESEQ[k];}catch(e){}});
    changed=true;
  }
  if(changed){save();if(typeof saveF==='function')saveF();if(typeof saveESeq==='function')saveESeq();}
}
(function clearNonOpsTS(){
  if(localStorage.getItem('shoptrans_tsops_v1'))return;
  try{
    Object.keys(XADD||{}).forEach(function(sheet){
      const arr=XADD[sheet];if(!Array.isArray(arr))return;
      const tsE=arr.find(function(x){return x&&x.ts;});if(!tsE)return;
      if(isOpsSheet(sheet))return;
      const tp=tsE.c;
      if(DATA[sheet]&&Array.isArray(DATA[sheet]))DATA[sheet].forEach(function(r){if(Array.isArray(r)&&tp<r.length)r.splice(tp,1);});
      const next=[];
      arr.forEach(function(x){
        if(x===tsE)return;
        const y=Object.assign({},x);
        if(typeof y.c==='number'&&y.c>tp)y.c=y.c-1;
        if(typeof y.after==='number'){if(y.after===tp)y.after=(tp-1>=0?tp-1:null);else if(y.after>tp)y.after=y.after-1;}
        next.push(y);
      });
      XADD[sheet]=next;
    });
    saveXAdd();if(typeof save==='function')save();
  }catch(e){}
  localStorage.setItem('shoptrans_tsops_v1','1');
})();
migrateFcf();
(function migrateExwCols(){
  var FLAG='shoptrans_exwcols_v2';
  try{if(localStorage.getItem(FLAG))return;}catch(_){}
  var cols=FLAT_COLS['EXW'];
  if(cols){
    function clr(st,k){try{if(st&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(st,k))delete st[k];}catch(_){}}
    var stores=[];
    try{if(typeof XADD!=='undefined')stores.push(XADD);}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof COLW!=='undefined')stores.push(COLW);}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof ROWH!=='undefined')stores.push(ROWH);}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof FMT!=='undefined')stores.push(FMT);}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof NOTES!=='undefined')stores.push(NOTES);}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof XFER!=='undefined')stores.push(XFER);}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof UCOL!=='undefined')stores.push(UCOL);}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof XHIDE!=='undefined')stores.push(XHIDE);}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof ESEQ!=='undefined')stores.push(ESEQ);}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof FREEZE!=='undefined')stores.push(FREEZE);}catch(_){}
    Object.keys(cols).forEach(function(dept){

          stores.forEach(function(st){clr(st,k);});
        });
      });
    });
    try{if(typeof save==='function')save();}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof saveXAdd==='function')saveXAdd();}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof saveW==='function')saveW();}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof saveH==='function')saveH();}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof saveF==='function')saveF();}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof saveNotes==='function')saveNotes();}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof saveXfer==='function')saveXfer();}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof saveUCol==='function')saveUCol();}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof saveXHide==='function')saveXHide();}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof saveESeq==='function')saveESeq();}catch(_){}

  try{if(typeof saveXAdd==='function')saveXAdd();}catch(_){}
  try{if(typeof saveW==='function')saveW();}catch(_){}
